SigrikaGo 系统设计

本文档是系统设计入口。详细设计已经按主题拆分到 docs/system-design/，修改架构、运行行为、资源体系、样式主题、部署方式或技术债时，应更新入口摘要或对应分篇，并运行 npm run docs:system-design 同步 HTML。

阅读顺序

1. 项目总览与功能边界 2. 前端架构与状态边界 3. 后端、HTTP API 与实时房间 4. 数据模型与领域系统 5. 资源、音频与启动预加载 6. UI 主题、移动端与交互体验 7. 性能、技术债与扩展路线 8. 工作流、文档规则与历史记录

当前架构摘要

  • 质量与稳定性基线：Playwright E2E/稳定性服务使用 .tmp/playwright/ 下的独立临时 SQLite 数据库并在退出时清理；GameRecord 按双方用户+时间、模式+计分状态+时间建立查询索引，个人/他人棋谱通过 (createdAt, id) 游标每次加载 50 盘并可继续读取完整历史，排行榜和成就兼容扫描仍有明确上限；本人履历与公开详细信息复用同一个服务端计分战绩统计，不再从已加载棋谱推导。所有 /api 未捕获异常由末端 JSON 错误中间件统一输出。登录会话将 lastSeenAt 写入节流为五分钟，refresh token 使用旧哈希 compare-and-swap 原子轮换；登录/注册凭据限流与 refresh/logout 会话限流使用独立桶，单实例内同一用户的 session replacement 按用户串行并在 Prisma 事务中完成撤销与新建。管理员身份只以数据库 User.role 为准，公开注册、登录、refresh 和启动任务都不按用户名自动提权；服务器操作员使用 npm run admin:promote -- <username> 幂等提升已存在账号。玩家弹窗可逐步接入共享 ModalDialog，获得 dialog 语义、焦点循环/恢复和嵌套 Escape 优先级；npm run check 现在先运行 ESLint，并以 jsdom + Testing Library 覆盖真实弹窗键盘交互。
  • 前端使用 React 19、Vite 和 Socket.IO client；src/main.jsx 只负责浏览器挂载，应用组合根在 src/app/App.jsx，业务状态逐步下沉到 src/app/* hooks 和独立视图组件，应用级弹窗可见性集中在 src/app/useOverlayState.js，房间/回放/结果弹窗会话状态集中在 src/app/useRoomSessionState.js，已关闭结果不会因 room:resume 再弹，匹配等待/成功过渡状态集中在 src/app/useMatchSessionState.js；移动端对弈模式选择弹窗将路数+时间和贴目/规则分成稳定两行，设置弹窗在竖屏下保持标题不被裁切、内部 tab 单行并列且面板区域独立滚动，资料详情对局回放使用固定头部+内部滚动列表，内层 replay table 不再作为滚动容器以免截断触摸滚动链；移动端图标按钮和关闭按钮由最终安全层兜底为 44px 触控目标，主题动效避免宽度布局动画和弹性曲线，邮箱未读数量在菜单按钮上作为绝对定位角标呈现，不参与按钮文字排版；开发期 Vite /socket.io 代理会静默处理后端 watch 重启造成的预期 websocket 断连错误。
  • 初始前端入口继续避免一次性加载低频生产工具和业务弹窗：AppRoutes 通过 React.lazy/Suspense 延迟加载后台管理台、剧情教学对弈路由和对局资源加载页，AppOverlays 延迟加载棋舍、履历、成就、个性化、仓库、排行榜、观战、好友、商店、招募、设置、公告、邮箱和留言板等低频 overlay；后台管理 src/styles/admin.css 与剧情教学对弈 src/styles/room/tutorial-battle-screen.css 也分别由对应 lazy route 组件直接导入，随路由 CSS chunk 加载，不再进入首屏 src/styles.css / 共享 room.css；剧情教学目标光圈规则独立在 src/styles/room/tutorial-battle-screen/target-ring.css，避免动作按钮与目标标记继续堆进同一个大文件；匹配、结果、toast、约战、真实房间 gameplay 和剧情播放器保持同步入口，避免影响对局与故事关闭/回退路径。客户端新增 src/app/playableReadyPreload.js 作为对局前预热层：登录到大厅后在 idle 时预热对局加载页 chunk，桌面端匹配入口 hover/focus 与模式按钮 hover/focus 会主动预热，真正点击匹配时按模式只为技能开启的 spark 预热 Pixi；Vite 开发期把 pixi.js 与 pixi.js/unsafe-eval 排除出 dep optimizer，同时显式 include pixi.js > @xmldom/xmldom、pixi.js > eventemitter3、pixi.js > gifuct-js 和 pixi.js > ismobilejs 让 Pixi 源码路径里的 CommonJS/conditional-export 子依赖继续走预打包 ESM 互操作，避免 Pixi 懒加载 WebGLRenderer 等 renderer 模块时命中浏览器缓存里的过期 .vite/deps chunk，也避免 raw CJS 子依赖缺少 default/named export 让技能 canvas 卡在 preparing；该层只改善本地缓存和首帧准备，不替代 room:preload-ready 服务端屏障。
  • 登录关键图片集合从共享模式元数据派生星炬、标准、五子棋首页铭牌图标，铭牌 DOM 同时使用同步解码提示，避免第三枚模式图标依赖悬停或点击重绘后才出现。
  • 剧情播放器在桌面端和移动端共享 StoryPlayerModal 交互语义：外层遮罩点击、右上关闭入口和快进/跳过入口都先打开同一个跳过/关闭确认层，只有确认按钮才执行实际关闭；当前台词仍在逐字显示时，点击剧情窗口内任意区域会立即显示完整文本，但不会推进节点或触发关闭。播放器按脚本节点预加载角色立绘，并以角色与资源地址保持同角色连续台词的立绘实例稳定；教学对弈的新 NPC 节点则原子替换说话人、立绘、配色和文本，避免台词已切换但仍显示上一角色。移动端教学气泡降低背景底板、边框与阴影的不透明度，用户选项降低背景底板的不透明度；姓名、正文、选项文字和立绘保持不透明，也不使用会模糊棋盘的背景滤镜。
  • Mobile root back handling is centralized in src/app/modalDismissal.js: login, preload, home, and admin parent screens show the 确定要退出游戏吗？ confirmation when no functional modal is open, while a loaded room forwards the back intent to RoomScreen so replay/spectator views return directly home and an unfinished player game reuses the existing “是否认输并退出房间？” confirmation. Close/cancel buttons and phone back inside functional modals still dismiss only the current modal and do not cascade into either exit path.
  • 后端使用 Express、Socket.IO、Prisma 和 SQLite；server/index.js 负责 HTTP/Socket 入口组合，启动数据与 schema 初始化收口在 server/serverStartup.js，Socket 连接事件套件由 server/socketEvents.js 统一装配，速率保护与匹配、房间连接/恢复、对局/数子/求和/计分、聊天、约战、断线清理等行为继续分布在对应 server/socket*Events.js 模块，生产静态托管收口在 server/staticAssets.js，认证、商城、抽卡、社交、回放、房间生命周期等已拆为领域模块；匹配或约战创建的房间先进入 preloading 阶段，由 room:preload-ready 形成双方资源就绪屏障，双方都 ready 后才进入 opening/自动猜先，任一方超过 90 秒未 ready 时服务端发出 match:preload-timeout 并中止匹配。
  • 服装系统由独立 Costume 目录、UserCostume 所有权和 UserCostumeEquipment 角色槽位组成；残星会服装店与扎希拉商铺在同一窗口内平移切换，但各自维护最多五件商品和独立 3 秒刷新批次，移动端首页宽度安全规则不得压缩这条双页滑轨。服装页标题固定为不换行的“残星会cosplay部”；残星会主体使用两张独立构图的深红蜡笔舞台背景：桌面 1586x992 图按左侧约 34% 娜波摩接待区、右侧约 65% 商品舞台组织，移动 941x1672 图按上方约 57% 商品区、下方约 43% 接待区组织，最终移动层直接切换竖图而不是裁切横图。背景从残星会会监弗洛洛的官方视觉中提炼深红帷幕、象牙服装、旧金舞台、破碎黄铜乐器、玻璃碎片与彼岸花语言，但使用与扎希拉商店一致的粗蜡笔颗粒、粗糙纸纹、歪斜轮廓和简化色块，不包含官方人物、文字或 UI；旧 CSS 幕布伪元素被关闭。残星会商品舞台复用扎希拉的容器测量、1–5 件数量感知布局、批次轻微旋转和纵向浮动参数，按当前商品数动态居中且不保留空槽，服装立绘与右下售价作为同一张商品卡同步变换。商品售价由随透明裁边立绘收缩的 .costume-shop-art 锚定在可见人物右下角并少量叠压，售价牌使用比旧粉灰底更明亮的浅粉肉色，桌面悬停保留位移动效但不铺粉色卡底，只在立绘后方显出局部暖金舞台光。两家商店复用同一个 ShopWallet 钱包图片、金币胶囊和排版组件，仅由各自页面决定位置；两位看板娘的购买反馈台词和立绘不再由计时器自动恢复，只在刷新、重试或关闭商店等显式操作时改变。残星会服装详情复用扎希拉商品详情的图像、分类、标题/画师、说明和底部操作槽结构，但通过服装专属修饰类保留深红金色主题；标签按所属角色显示“{角色名}服装”，底部操作槽以左对齐售价行加下一行完整宽度购买按钮替代持有状态。购买请求无论成功失败都会关闭详情，只有成功时才由 ShopModal 打开独立居中的立即装扮确认窗口。购买与金币扣除在事务内完成，部员手册可随时查看角色全部服装并即时装扮；服装选择与服装详情分别作为应用根下的同级顶层覆盖窗口，详情不嵌入或裁在衣柜窗口内部。衣柜标题固定为“{角色名}的衣柜”，不显示副标题或只有默认服装的解释；服装立绘触发区保持透明且无额外阴影，当前服装只由外层卡片的浅绿色背景表达。每件服装可在后台配置显示缩放与横纵偏移，校准只作用于衣柜缩略图和实际装扮后的角色立绘，商店商品图与服装详情保持原始画面。角色立绘统一经过服装/糖果优先级解析。正式对局在房间创建时固化双方服装 id、立绘 URL 与显示校准，并写入 GameRecord，因此后续换装或后台调整不会改写进行中房间或历史棋谱。后台“服装管理”维护服装元数据、显示校准、上下架与糖果特效候选立绘，停用服装只清除当前装备、不删除所有权。
  • 后台角色管理响应以 id 暴露规范角色标识；服装编辑器用该 id 作为列表匹配、选项值和提交的 characterSlug，仅为旧响应形状保留 slug 回退，避免把角色显示名误提交为服装目标。
  • 星炬模式提供服务端权威的“准时宝”人机练习：客户端通过绝对定位在“星炬对弈”标题右上方、水平中心线与卡片上边框平齐的 /assets/home/home-practice-zhunshibao.webp 透明图标签直接开局；标签保留“准时宝陪练”可访问名称，桌面/竖屏宽度分别为 144px / 136px。Bright School 最终效果层让该透明图复用主页左下工具图片按钮的硬边手绘阴影：常态/悬停/聚焦为 6px 8px 0 rgba(61, 43, 37, 0.42)，按下为 4px 5px 0 rgba(61, 43, 37, 0.34)，覆盖共享模态层的柔影后备。悬停/键盘聚焦动效由按钮级 CSS 变量合成居中、抬升与顺时针 7 度旋转，并在 Bright School 最终按钮胜出层完整引用这些变量，避免通用 button > * 子元素重置吞掉图片旋转；旋转轴锚在左侧准时宝主体附近，使右侧牌面清晰摆动。减少动态效果时仅取消过渡、保留即时悬停状态。入口不再打开二次选择窗，统一发送 practice:start 的基础难度与随机黑白配置；接口仍保留入门/基础和黑/白/随机参数能力。练习房固定 rated=false、matchSource=practice、recordPolicy=none，复用正式预加载、开局、5 分钟与 30 秒 3 次读秒、技能、数子、断线恢复和运行快照；共享执色开局窗在人机房的“本局你执黑/白”下方额外显示红色胜利提示，基础档为“吃掉准时宝22颗棋子就算胜利！”，恢复旧入门房时按配置显示 11，真人局不显示。虚拟玩家 zhunshibao 无角色与技能并自动 ready，展示段位统一为“入门陪练”，但 basic / beginner 内部难度和 22/11 子阈值保持独立；其结果弹窗不再重复显示“不计成长/不保存棋谱”说明。准时宝使用 /assets/characters/zhunshibao.png 透明立绘；立绘存在时不套用教学无角色框或机器人专属缩放，而是继承普通角色桌面 --side-portrait 与竖屏 46px 头像几何。其 PlayerInfo 同时复用不可见评分和技能占位，保持与普通角色信息区相同的桌面及竖屏结构高度，但不显示虚假评分或技能。练习房计入活跃房间容量，但不进入匹配人数、队列或公开观战列表，也不生成 GameRecord、奖励、成长和任务进度。准时宝只从自身 gameViewForColor 按紧急吃逃、最近局部、开局点和棋形理由生成至多 48 个候选，以正式 playMove 做一层战术安全检查，并过滤自打吃、填眼、空三角和非战斗性的早期直线延伸；前两手普通落子优先占据两个不同角星位，入门随机也只在接近最佳分的合理候选内选择。低价值候选仅在空交叉点不超过有效棋盘的 12%（且阈值至少为 8 点）时允许弃手，不再按手数提前弃手。入门/基础分别在玩家普通提子达到 11/22 子后于下一次行动前认输，skillRemovals 不计入阈值。
  • Prisma Migrate 的生产历史以完整 SQLite 0_init 为唯一基线：新库直接 migrate deploy，已有预上线库必须停服备份并通过 schema diff 后再显式 migrate resolve --applied 0_init；npm run verify:migrations 在隔离临时库验证空库部署、旧库无损接管、重复 deploy 和 schema 一致性，应用启动不会自动修改迁移历史。
  • 阶段 3 把上线保障收敛为 npm run verify:release-candidate：依次生成 Prisma Client、验证迁移、检查生产配置、构建、运行 desktop/mobile 稳定性、演练 SQLite 备份恢复并执行容量 smoke，失败立即停止。npm run check:production 与服务端启动使用相同的 dotenv/config 入口读取当前工作目录 .env，因此独立检查和一键更新脚本不会漏掉 systemd EnvironmentFile 中的真实密钥与站点 origin。稳定性夹具通过仅限 NODE_ENV=stability|capacity 且显式启用测试 action 的认证路由准备角色/容量管理员，生产注册、角色默认所有权与管理员权限不受影响；稳定性限流 namespace 也只在 stability 环境读取受控请求头。backup:sqlite 使用显式源/目标与 SQLite 一致性副本，自动恢复演练被限制在 .tmp/backup-restore/；发布手册定义了内测前 30 分钟、随后 24 小时的健康/ack/event-loop/RSS/恢复/持久化观察线与回滚分支。
  • 单实例生产运行新增显式排空与容量边界：server/runtimeServiceState.js 以 MAX_ONLINE_USERS=500、MAX_ACTIVE_ROOMS=100、MAX_SPECTATORS_PER_ROOM=20 为可配置软上限，达到上限时只拒绝新匹配、约战和新观战，已在房间中的玩家及原观战者仍可恢复；/health/live 与 /health/ready 分离存活和接流状态。SIGINT/SIGTERM 或受管子进程 shutdown 消息会先进入 drain 并通知客户端，再关闭 Socket.IO/HTTP、强制刷新房间快照、停止性能采样并断开 Prisma，整个过程有 15 秒硬超时。客户端 game:action 使用稳定 actionId、ack 和最多两次同 ID 重试，服务端把有限去重回执随房间快照持久化，确保 ack 丢失不会造成重复落子；真实子进程重启测试覆盖落子、停机、同库启动和玩家恢复。Phase 2 将观战黑方视图固定在 game、只把白方差异视图放入 gameViews.white，房间创建只发送包含创建通知的 match:found 权威快照，大厅统计通过 100ms trailing debounce 合并连接/断开风暴。
  • Server entrypoints run prisma generate before dev:server and start so Prisma Client stays aligned with prisma/schema.prisma after schema edits such as StoryScript initial-board columns. On Windows, an already-running Node server can still hold Prisma's query engine DLL open; stop the old server before regenerating or restarting.
  • 后台管理新增面向云端运营的分析入口：概况 默认呈现“今日简报”，用 正常 / 需要关注 / 需要处理 总状态、原因、分级解读和下一步动作替代密集报表；运营分析 展示最近日期范围的活跃、注册、对局、玩家分层、经济和模式趋势。后端聚合逻辑集中在 server/adminAnalytics.js，通过 /api/admin/analytics/overview 与 /api/admin/analytics/operations 输出可读摘要，实时在线与匹配/房间统计从 onlineSessions、rooms.js 运行时边界注入，深度事件暂以 待接入 标注。
  • 后台管理页是独立的全屏桌面工作台：.admin-screen 在后台样式层覆盖基础 1280px 页面宽度限制，使用视口全宽、固定侧栏和可滚动主工作区，避免剧情教学等高密度工具被压窄；最终 mobile-adaptive/admin-fullscreen.css 会在主题/HUD 层之后重置 .app-shell:has(.admin-screen) 的浅色背景并隐藏游戏化伪背景，确保后台不继承大厅或 HUD 的黑绿外观。
  • 角色目录支持后台维护配音人员展示信息：Character.cvName 与可选 cvUrl 通过 admin/public character payload 下发，棋舍角色详情在桌面把 CV：{cvName} 放在角色名右侧，在移动端放在角色名下方；链接只接受 http(s) 或站内路径，并保持继承色、无下划线、无 visited/hover 变色，避免影响标题和 BGM 播放器布局。商城商品同样支持后台维护商品图插画署名：ShopItem.illustName 与可选 illustUrl 会在商品详情标题区显示 illust：{illustName}，移动端堆叠在商品名下方，链接态与纯文本态保持同色、无下划线、无边框、无背景和稳定行高。
  • 角色技能结构与逻辑由代码和角色默认数据管理，后台角色页只允许修改基础技能及代码已定义派生技能的名称、描述和超频；效果类型、目标规则、使用次数、回合行为、参数、音乐绑定、启用状态以及派生技能增删都不可由后台改变。派生技能只从当前基础技能显式的 params.derivedSkills[] 读取，通用草稿/解析器不再注入爱弥斯“远航星”；启动同步只补代码新增的缺失定义且保留运营文案覆盖，一次性迁移会清理旧版本误写到千咲等其他角色上的默认“远航星”。
  • 技能说明把固定超频与可复用文案语义分层：角色详情从基础/派生技能的 costValue 生成红字浅红底 超频：X 徽标，对局说明以红色 超频：X 独立首行展示，仇远、千咲等动态技能仍显示结构化基础值 0，动态公式留在正文。描述中的人工 【特性词】 引用由共享渲染器按原文顺序解析为可点击、可聚焦的下划线文本词条，保留原生按钮语义但显式清除主题按钮底板、边框和阴影；粗指针触控热区由绝对定位伪元素扩展，按钮本身保持 min-height: 0 和正文行高，不能撑大段落行距，未知词典项安全退化为普通文本。玩家端词典使用共享同步快照：确认过的内置词条作为首帧兜底，服务端成功响应后原子替换快照并通知所有已挂载说明；失败请求不进入永久缓存，保留最近成功值或内置兜底并自动重试，因此移动技能提示层临时挂载时不会先把已确认词条渲染成不可点击文字。手机角色详情由弹窗壳自身承担纵向滚动并从内容顶部排布，避免长技能说明被固定高度与 overflow: hidden 截断。对局与回放按角色 ID 优先读取当前前端角色目录的基础/派生展示文案，房间或回放快照只在目录缺失时兜底，派生技能仍保留快照中的次数和来源状态；词条说明从触发点锚定、视口钳制，并把房间浮层基准至少钳到移动技能说明壳的层级后再提升，确保解释层可见且关闭解释时保留下层技能详情。SkillTrait 是只承载名称/释义/排序的持久化文案模型，后台独立“特性词”页负责 CRUD、引用查看和受保护删除，角色基础/派生描述编辑器可在光标处插入；重命名在同一事务中精确替换引用，保存描述拒绝未知或重复词条，但特性词从不驱动技能规则、可用性、回合或结算。
  • 公告中心和邮箱是登录玩家的两个独立入口，但共用 InformationCenterLayout 阅读骨架与 ModalDialog 可访问性边界：桌面统一为约 1020px 的左侧列表/右侧阅读区，移动端在同一近全屏窗口内从列表进入详情并通过明确返回按钮回到原列表。桌面公告、更新日志和邮件加载完成后默认打开列表第一条，并沿用既有打开即已读路径；切换公告分类也会打开该分类第一条，桌面空列表显示“这里空空如也~”。移动端保持列表优先，只有点击条目后才进入详情并标记已读，空列表继续使用各领域简洁文案。共享样式由 src/styles/modals/information-center.css 与最终 src/styles/mobile-adaptive/information-center.css 负责尺寸、安全区、主从切换、约 260ms 的 transform/opacity 平滑减速动效和 reduced-motion；移动标题栏固定预留返回控件槽，避免切换时标题与关闭按钮重排，桌面空阅读提示不会参与移动端详情退出动画。公告/邮箱领域样式继续分别拥有列表状态、正文纸张、附件与动作，邮箱阅读区只保留邮件纸本身的单层边框。Bright School 使用纸张网格外壳、暖白阅读面和粉色选择/未读侧标，不再通过整卡大面积状态染色或嵌套详情弹窗表达层级。后台公告和邮箱管理、服务端 API、AnnouncementEntry/AnnouncementRead 与邮箱数据模型均不变。
  • 首页公告未读红点在桌面顶栏和移动菜单里都属于公告按钮自身的状态标记；移动端必须像邮箱角标一样以按钮为定位上下文贴到右上角，不能作为菜单行末尾的静态 flex 内容。
  • 对局模式由 src/shared/gameModes.js 统一配置，前后端共享模式顺序、棋盘大小、贴目、技能开关、时间控制、图标路径和模式选择英文标签；三种模式图标位于 public/assets/match-modes/，路径为 /assets/match-modes/mode-spark.png、/assets/match-modes/mode-gomoku.png 和 /assets/match-modes/mode-standard.png。首页铭牌按星炬、标准、五子棋三列显示图标和段位数字，不再显示积分；视觉层只显示 rank 数字，完整“段/级”语义留在 aria-label，铭牌专属 CSS 会给三列图标和 WuWa 数字使用更大的显示槽位，避免复用匹配水印尺寸时显得过小。点击匹配入口后的模式选择按钮保留左侧中文标题/规则和右侧人数，同时通过指针透明的居中水印层显示“图标 + WuWa 英文标签”，桌面透明度为 50%，移动端透明度为 20%。
  • Bright School 首页的图片式匹配/手册入口和 2x3 工具按钮依赖 WebP 透明图像加 drop-shadow 表现纸片厚度；匹配入口 fantasy-match-entry 当前 alpha 裁切尺寸为 2374x1960，部员手册入口 book-entry 为 705x850；桌面和移动端都必须保持这些入口、工具 grid 与按钮容器 overflow: visible，工具按钮图像自身保留右/下透明 bleed，避免阴影被卡片或最终移动安全层裁切。
  • 招募道具运行期图标通过 src/shared/recruitment.js 同步给招募、商城和仓库等消费者；后端商品与背包 payload 会把内置招募道具的旧落库 imageUrl 归一到当前共享配置，启动回填只匹配每个道具声明的空值或旧默认 SVG 路径。“招新贴报/招新海报”使用 public/assets/items/recruitment-poster.webp，由 C:/codex/image/item/Recruitment_paper.png 无损转换而来；“先约电台广播券”使用 public/assets/items/radio-recruitment-ticket.webp，由 C:/codex/image/item/boardcast_ticket.png 转换而来。“彩虹豆豆跳跳糖”的运行期道具图标使用 public/assets/items/rainbow-bean-candy.webp，由 C:/codex/image/item/rainbow-candy-cutout-full.png 无损转换而来；server/itemImages.js 统一让商城商品、仓库道具和玩家侧抽卡奖项/结果归一到当前 WebP，默认快照中的糖果商品和卡池奖励也使用同一路径。public/assets/promotional/ 仍保留未接入运行时引用的候选宣传图，例如“招新贴报”候选 recruitment-poster-v1..v3.png 与“先约电台广播券”候选 radio-coupon-v1..v3.png，候选图不进入启动预加载清单，除非后续 UI 明确引用。
  • 爱弥斯不再属于新用户初始角色；注册事务会同时创建“飞行雪绒歌友会”系统邮件并附带 1 张仅限招募使用的“飞行雪绒纪念券”，旧用户则通过一次性启动迁移保留爱弥斯。纪念券道具图使用从原始透明 PNG 有色边界等比裁切得到的 512×436 WebP /assets/items/aemeath-flight-snow-memorial-ticket.webp，因此在招募按钮、仓库和邮件附件中与另外两种招募道具保持接近的有效画面占比。玩家邮箱把道具附件投影为物品图、中文名称与数量，不直接显示内部 itemId；领取后动作按钮在所有交互状态下保持灰色。新手引导关闭、完成或跳过后，POST /api/onboarding-story/exited 以 User.welcomeMailNoticeShownAt 保证浅绿色“你有收到新的邮件！” toast 只提示一次。纪念券在仓库可见、玩家商店隐藏、后台邮箱附件可选；持券时招募底栏按共享排序插入具有明亮高对比内层的五彩动态按钮，道具数量为零的动作统一显示“数量不足”。后台“招募管理”通过 recruitmentConfig.fixedItemTexts 修改纪念券招募说明和爱弥斯招募台词：玩家状态只获得有效说明，新发起任务把当时的台词快照到 RecruitmentTask.responseText，不会提前泄露结果文案；选择卡在说明与红色状态文案相同时只显示红色文案，避免同一句话重复出现。特殊招募由服务端固定爱弥斯和 11.25 秒领取 readyAt；新任务响应到达客户端时另建完整 11.25 秒展示期限，避免移动端响应耗时吃掉演出尾段。前端在不可操作的 7.05 秒演出中先显示持续运行的 999 分钟表演倒计时，从首帧开始渐暗，白光从小人停驻位置扩散，于 6.25 秒全白遮挡下切换到 5 秒展示倒计时，并同步播放 /assets/music/aemeath-recruitment-full-white-burst.ogg；演出完成只解除交互锁，展示期限归零的同一节拍直接进入 ready，不停在 00:00 等网络刷新，领取接口仍以更早开始的服务端 readyAt 校验。刷新、离线或页面隐藏才会调用中断接口让任务立即可领取，不补演。演出素材支持静态/动态 WebP 与精灵图集两个稳定资源槽，当前使用 /assets/recruitment/aemeath-pink-cyber-angel-spritesheet.webp 的右飞行八帧和招手四帧；BGM 从渐暗首帧以 350ms 降至 15%，解锁时以 500ms 恢复。
  • 玩家侧艺术字体作为语义化、白名单式资源使用：public/assets/fonts/WuWa-Lahai-Roi-Regular.ttf 通过 "Sigrika Accent Latin" 字体族挂到 --font-display-accent 与 --font-numeric-accent，只覆盖拉丁字母和数字。倒计时数字必须经 .text-clock-value，玩家可见积分/rating 数值必须经 .text-rating-value，登录页 SigrikaGo 英文品牌短标签、首页品牌/模式选择英文水印/锁定占位等氛围短标签可经 .text-display-accent；这些艺术字语义类用 text-transform: uppercase 让小写拉丁字母视觉显示为大写，但不改源字符串和数据。用户名、后台管理、正文、表单、金币/价格/库存、局数胜负、房间号、时间戳和排行榜名次不使用该字体，后续替换字体只改 token/asset 而不改语义类名。最终 mobile-adaptive/semantic-accent-typography.css 在主题层之后回补这三个语义类，避免 Bright School 的广域 !important 字体/颜色重置压掉棋钟读秒颜色和 rating 数字字体，并清除 .text-clock-value 误继承的徽章背景、边框和阴影；主时间棋钟数字固定为 #1c171a 而不是灰色，读秒警告状态保留红色/多色语义，移动端房间玩家条内会把艺术字体棋钟压到更小字号与宽度，避免读秒条被挤出容器。
  • 首页主标题和各弹窗/窗口的 h2 标题使用内置 public/assets/fonts/LXGWMarkerGothic-Regular.ttf，由 "Sigrika Window Title" 与 --font-window-title 单点声明；首页标题通过 .text-window-title 显式使用，窗口标题通过 dialog/backdrop 语义边界应用。正文、副标题、按钮和数据字体不随之改变，最终 mobile-adaptive/semantic-accent-typography.css 会在 Bright School 广域字体重置之后重新应用该标题 token；拥有更高专属选择器的窗口（如 .shop-header h2）必须在自身 owner CSS 中继续引用同一 token，不能重新硬编码微软雅黑。
  • Capture-removal counters may be negative and still participate in scoring: Lynae spray-stone and QiuYuan row-slash subtract 1 removal from the acting player when the skill succeeds, while ChangLi double-move adds 1 removal after a completed second placement.
  • 当前内置对局模式为 spark、standard 和 gomoku。五子棋沿用 13 路棋盘与星位、5 分钟 30 秒 3 次读秒、自动猜先和独立 UserModeStats(mode=gomoku) 段位/积分；需要出战角色但 skillEnabled=false，房间 UI 不显示弃手、数子、提子、除子、超频或技能信息。 Gomoku five-in-row results carry winner.winningLine; the board keeps a persistent gold highlight on those five stones, live games show the result modal immediately, and replay only shows the highlight.
  • 对局结果弹窗是当前玩家视角而不是胜方公告牌：ResultModal 通过 resultPlayerForRoom 取当前用户的房间玩家作为 displayPlayer，胜负/平局/观战只改变 result-outcome-* 状态、文案和视觉效果；胜利显示 赢了耶！ 并保留角色跳动，失败显示 输掉了...、灰度冻结当前用户立绘，并叠加 /assets/effects/result-defeat-rain.gif 雨云效果。结果奖励中的 rating 数值继续使用 .text-rating-value，金币和用户名不使用艺术数字字体；奖励卡片必须有实色语义底，rating 负值用浅红、非负值用浅绿，金币用浅金色，rating 数值内层 span 只继承父奖励卡片底色，不再绘制独立背景、边框或阴影。
  • 站点公开配置通过 SiteSetting key/value 存储，并由 src/shared/siteSettings.js 定义前后端共享默认值；后台系统设置可编辑大厅标题/副标题、关于文本、首页 footer 长文本、加载页提示语集合和全局技能特效开关，HomeHeader 从公开配置消费 homeTitle 与 homeSubtitle，副标题通过 text-display-accent 让拉丁/数字走 WuWa 艺术字体且中文回退到 UI 字体，首页在线人数以透明图标+数字 HUD 标签呈现；留言板输入框默认使用更高的写作区域，移动端再按视口高度收敛；footer 支持受限 Markdown 链接，加载页提示语按行维护并在玩家预加载时随机轮播；加载屏内容在桌面、移动端和短视口中保持整屏居中，标题与提示语允许按容器宽度换行，中间加载面板保持透明且不覆盖整页主题背景。共享加载轨道改为桌面 20px、手机 18px 的奶油色手绘纸带，完整橙黄蜡笔填色、短划痕、纸面留白和暖色底光保持固定纹理尺寸，仅通过 mask-size 与 clip-path 回退从左向右揭示，不再按进度拉伸。小橘子高度仍为轨道三倍，沿揭示边缘在 0–100% 累计顺时针滚动两圈；位置、滚动和停顿 600ms 后的轻微呼吸由三层 transform owner 分离。reduced-motion 保持正确端点和揭示范围，但关闭插值、滚动与呼吸。Bright School 注册提交态为 .auth-password-toggle:disabled 保留 translateY(-50%) 的组件所有权，避免通用禁用按钮规则让显示密码按钮下滑。
  • ratingRules controls dynamic rated-match rewards, rank-gap scaling, optional anti-boosting, rank-change rating deltas, and friendly-match coin limits. Matchmaking is rated; direct/private duels are friendly, excluded from public stats/rank windows, kept in replay history, and marked in replay UI.
  • 对局规则支持黑白棋子与命名中立棋子并存；中立棋子按同名阵营连接、阻断领地并参与气/提子判定，但不归属黑白除子。src/shared/game.js 保留共享对局 API 门面，阶段常量、动作结果、普通落子/隐藏手动作、主动技能动作和技能状态变更 helper 已拆到 gamePhases.js、gameActionResult.js、gameStoneActions.js、gameSkillActions.js 和 gameSkillState.js，降低新增技能继续膨胀核心状态机的风险。
  • 默认普通棋子使用棋盘交叉点尺寸的 84%，共享基础层与 Bright School 后置定位层保持相同尺寸合同；默认白棋使用暖灰象牙色径向明暗而非纯白高光，装饰棋子仍由独立图片资源完整接管背景，不继承普通棋子的配色。
  • 星炬技能体系包含达妮娅（denia）默认名为“泡影幻梦”的 flip-stone 主动技能、面向整行的 row-slash 主动技能、面向空交叉点的 protocol-takeover 主动技能和长离（changli）double-move 连落技能；教程和房间技能横幅的 fallback 角色配置也必须使用“泡影幻梦”，避免旧名污染无 API 角色配置的本地教学对局。仇远（qiuyuan）通过“一斩足矣”指定任意有效交叉点，移除该横线所有棋子并按直接移除数增加超频，莫宁（mornye）通过“协议接管”把一个空点写入对手禁入协议，管理员在部员招募系统实装前默认拥有这两个部员招募角色；仇远 row-slash 在技能横幅结束后通过 BoardSkillEffects 播放不依赖外部纹理的 Pixi Graphics 青白水墨刀光：两道竖向贯穿预兆刀光（第一道自上向下、第二道自下向上扫过，角度在 -30 到 30 度间确定性随机，并留存到主刀光收势时一起淡出）和 1.8 格高主刀都去掉浅蓝透明厚波纹，改为白色直刃叠加青白渐变边光、深青墨/灰蓝墨短段不规则水墨涂抹边缘，随后从左到右刷过目标行并同步触发 row-slash-cut-pending 棋子消失；BoardRowSlashOverlay 让最终 .board-row-slash 以超出棋盘两侧的长度随 Pixi 主刀同一时间线从左向右裁切展开，casting 时序由 boardEffectDurationMs * 0.19 延迟和 * 0.22 持续时间驱动，刀痕起点略向下偏移以让倾斜刀痕中点交合目标行中线，结算后的同类刀痕继续来自 game.rowEffects，并通过 clearAfterColor 在对手下一次行动后清除；莫宁 protocol-takeover 在技能横幅后通过 BoardSkillEffects 播放淡紫与冰蓝数据流光束从上方向目标点落下的 Pixi 演出，并同步播放更明显的两段式数据束启动与目标锁定 SFX，结算后禁入点继续显示淡紫与冰蓝 DOM/CSS 协议标记；长离 double-move 在技能横幅后通过 BoardSkillEffects 播放透明 SVG 红色火焰凤凰飞入与不规则边界的全棋盘火焰 Pixi 演出，随后连续落下的棋子写入 double-move-stone 并继续显示复用透明火焰 sprite 的 DOM 持续光效；琳奈 spray-stone 生成的喷涂中立棋子使用 /assets/stones/spray-stone.webp 透明 WebP 作为 .spray .stone 背景，资源归入技能效果预加载组；娜波摩 color-illusion-passive 在被动持续期间由 BoardAmbientEffects 播放 pointer-transparent 的中心向外 CSS 灰白化扩散波纹，并由 .board-wrap.color-illusion-board-surface::before 从棋盘中心扩散切换到 /assets/boards/nabomo-color-illusion-board.webp 灰白木纹背景，该背景保持到被动结束，避免使用持续 Pixi 烟雾或被边界裁切的雾层；该被动通过技能演出配置层声明 2 秒横幅结束即结算，避免横幅后空档；src/shared/skillPresentation.js 统一解析技能演出时间线、棋盘/Pixi/DOM 图层能力和全局关闭特效开关，共享棋盘 CSS 通过 import-only src/styles/room/board.css 分派到 src/styles/room/board/，棋盘点位按钮使用 --board-point-center-x/y 绝对锚到 SVG 网格交叉点并保持透明无按钮皮肤，.board-lines SVG 网格层也显式保持铺满棋盘和可见 stroke，避免全局/主题按钮或媒体规则盖住、压塌棋盘网格。
  • 西格莉卡 erase-point 结算后的无效交叉点使用 /assets/effects/sigrika-erased-field-marker.webp 透明 WebP 作为 point-local 坑洞标记，.void 以 1.5 个棋盘格子长度显示，pending 陨石命中揭示和桌面/移动端棋盘共用同一尺寸合同。
  • Lynae spray-stone pending previews include all transformed points in affectedPointIds so the Pixi cast covers the selected target and resolved random target together; Board masks those original stones with .spray-transform-pending during the post-banner effect window, then .spray-transform-pending .stone keeps the original stone visible while an internal borderless cyan/purple/pink/yellow irregular spray-paint cover blooms above it, and switches to the same /assets/stones/spray-stone.webp artwork only after that cover has stayed opaque through most of the post-banner effect window, and .spray .stone keeps that WebP-only neutral spray stone with no CSS/SVG fallback stone, plus a point-bound colorful underside glow. During the pending paint-cover frame, Board derives the stone offset from the final spray stone type so the covered original stone and resolved neutral spray stone do not jump between positions.
  • Denia/Danya flip-stone keeps the Pixi bubble renderer on the normal post-banner board-effect layer, but its shared pending resolution delay is 3040ms instead of the default 4000ms: the authoritative flipped stone snapshot lands while the bubble is already dark and covering the target, before the pop/shard phase reveals the changed stone.
  • Local skill-animation GIF export is a development-only preview workflow, not a production asset pipeline. npm run export:skill-gifs -- --effect <effect> [--character <id>] runs scripts/export-skill-gifs.mjs, starts a local Vite harness under .trellis/tasks/06-23-gif/capture-harness, mounts the same BoardSkillEffects/Board presentation paths used in live rooms, captures frames with Playwright, and encodes looping GIFs with ffmpeg under outputs/skill-gifs/<character-or-effect>/. The exporter supports effect-only previews on the current .board-wrap board texture and full board-context previews, keeps audio muted, and uses representative mock board metadata instead of entering a real Socket.IO room. Captures run under Playwright virtual time: the harness preloads the real effect path before the cast starts, then advances the browser clock by exactly one frame per screenshot so slow screenshot I/O cannot compress a multi-second animation into a faster GIF. Board-context Voyage Star captures also switch their mock Board to the resolved crater/removal state during the full-white cover window, matching the live room's hidden-under-cover settlement path.
  • outputs/ and local .codex-dev*.log files are ignored development artifacts. Runtime assets that must ship should live under public/assets/** or another explicit source directory; generated GIF frames, preview screenshots, contact sheets, and local logs should not be tracked in Git.
  • 玩家资源包含角色、装饰、道具、音乐、抽卡奖励、蓝宝石和金币；结构化关系表与旧字符串字段仍处在兼容迁移期，legacy 解析、结构化同步和公开资产合并规则集中在 server/userAssets.js。
  • 部员手册角色详情 BGM 现在按普通技能与各派生技效果类型独立选曲：普通技继续使用 musicSelections.skill[characterId]，派生技使用 musicSelections.derivedSkill[characterId][effectType]，服务端按角色、效果类型和库存精确校验后只更新对应槽位。对局技能预览把玩法 effectType 与音乐槽位 musicEffectType 分离：普通技能的 musicEffectType 固定为空，派生技才携带派生效果类型，并把该字段保留到最新技能历史；旧派生历史缺少此字段时只按 musicTrackId 的音轨元数据兼容推断，避免 flip-stone、liberty-purge 等普通玩法效果被误读为派生音乐槽位。播放器共享磁带机结构、技能页签、单行溢出跑马灯、浮动选曲纸页和局部保存/播放错误恢复，Bright School 主题负责方格纸、硬阴影与 Rough.js 手绘皮肤；手机保留标题旁内联布局和 44px 播放触控目标。播放键和衣柜装扮按钮保留原有颜色、阴影、位移、按下态与键盘聚焦反馈，不额外创建预绘键面或合成提示层；角色详情的全屏嵌套遮罩改用静态半透明填充，不再实时模糊其背后页面。
  • Warehouse inventory uses a wider two-column collection grid on desktop and a single-column enlarged-art row layout on mobile. Each item card owns an explicit media mount and copy region: Bright School desktop media uses a 104px square mount, while mobile uses a clamp(64px, 20vw, 72px) mount so portrait posters, square items, and transparent-canvas tickets remain fully visible through object-fit: contain. Quantity is rendered as a shared ×N sticker anchored to the media corner on both viewports, and mobile cards increase name/description hierarchy without making the non-interactive article itself look pressable. Compact mobile use buttons may stay below the general 44px target when the warehouse composition needs the tighter proportion, but must remain readable and non-overlapping. Disabled warehouse use actions such as recruitment-only items render as gray native disabled buttons on desktop and mobile. Character-target item selection disables unavailable character cards with gray native disabled state only, without showing reason badges or reason text in the card/title.
  • Shop card purchase actions use native disabled buttons for every unavailable state, including sold out, already owned, not purchasable, pending purchase, and insufficient coins. Base shop CSS, Bright School shop polish, and final mobile overrides all render those disabled purchase buttons with the same gray inactive treatment instead of category-specific owned/sold-out colors.
  • 扎希拉商铺取消分类、分页和空占位卡，打开时从全部可售商品中生成最多五张会话内批次，隐藏已拥有的一次性商品和已售罄的用户库存商品；购买后当前卡片保留状态，下一次手动刷新才离场。初始批次和每次刷新都进入 3 秒冷却，冷却期间固定选定并有界预加载下一批，优先使用上一批未出现的商品，刷新本身不重新请求目录；加载、失败、空目录和购买反馈由扎希拉台词表达。商品卡左上角以“部员/道具/棋子/音乐”标记分类；右上角仅为消耗品显示剩余数量，不限量显示 ∞，限购 1 不显示数量角标。桌面和移动端统一按数量使用五件 2+3、四件 2+2、三件 2+1、一至两件单排居中的构图；桌面只在安全槽位内做有种子的轻微位置扰动并保留至少 28px 间距，批次内旋转固定，4–6px 基础浮动参数在桌面呈现为 8–12px 总行程，移动端仍维持 4–6px 总行程。Bright School 扎希拉商店主体改用两张独立构图的版本化蜡笔背景：桌面 /assets/shop/zahira-shop-background-crayon-v1.webp 为 1586x992，按左侧约 68% 商品陈列区、右侧接待/立绘区组织；竖屏 /assets/shop/zahira-shop-background-crayon-mobile-v1.webp 为 941x1672，按上方约 56% 商品区、下方约 44% 接待区组织，最终移动层直接选择竖图而非裁切横图。两张图都以居中 cover 覆盖 .zahira-store-page .shop-layout.shop-window-body，不再叠加旧伪元素背景、运行时滤镜或持续装饰动效；对应 PNG 作为源图保留，WebP 进入商店启动预加载。扎希拉 header 通过 data-store="zahira" 使用简单的蓝灰—灰紫低细节配色条，不再裁切或模拟主体场景，明确区分页头控件层与主画面；残星会 header 通过 data-store="costume" 复用其舞台图顶部帷幕，以深红、黑梅低对比蜡笔色块衔接主体舞台，标题使用暖象牙色，现有浅色纸面操作按钮继续保持清晰对比。移动端商店最终层清除通用模态框 padding/gap，使 header 与商品区使用完整纸张宽度，卡片保持约 4–5px 可见间隔且无内部滚动；关闭按钮在最终 owner 中恢复静态网格定位，与刷新按钮居中对齐且共同保持 44px 触控目标。商店专属卡片层同时解除竖屏全局 max-width、旧卡片最小高度与内容居中收缩污染，使槽位、缩放层和真实卡片共享同一尺寸合同。卡片内部以独立详情按钮承载放大的商品图片区、名称和价格，购买按钮作为同级原生按钮固定在底行并通过固定的全宽网格列使用一致内容宽度，售价元素自身水平居中，避免旧分类规则造成按钮宽度和价格对齐漂移；台词锚在扎希拉上方，移动钱包位于气泡右侧且避开可见头部。商铺继续使用两张 1448x1054 无损 WebP 立绘，并通过 SHOP_WALLET_IMAGE 引用独立 1024x768 透明钱包 WebP，金币数保持实时文本。
  • Player-facing character acquisition now uses the recruitment system instead of the gacha modal. Recruitment consumes warehouse items from the shop misc category, allows only one active task per user, decides the result at start time, exposes a five-minute pending period, shows the home recruitment entry pink ready background only for ready-to-view responses with a client-side readyAt refresh in addition to polling, grants a fixed-code-pool character on success, refunds the consumed item on no-response, computes remaining candidates from the merged legacy-plus-structured user asset projection, and rejects start immediately without consuming the item when the selected item has no remaining recruitable candidates. Non-production recruitment builds expose /api/recruitment/fast-forward and a clock icon button beside the countdown to shorten the pending task to five seconds; production rejects this route and the production UI hides the test shortcut. The recruitment modal main panel uses the flat Bright School-compatible /assets/recruitment/notice-board-flat-candidate.webp bulletin-board background for idle, pending, ready, and result states; the image is scaled proportionally with background-size: cover and vertically centered rather than stretched. The empty no-item prompt may still use the cropped /assets/recruitment/recruitment-letter-paper-flat.webp stationery paper, but item-backed selection, pending, ready, and no-response/result cards keep their state-card surfaces image-free behind the active item watermark or item/portrait content so the recruit item artwork is not overlapped by the stationery paper image. The selected-item confidence line is a red secondary cue, pending recruitment hides the helper label and renders only the remaining time digits as CRT-style green glowing tabular text without a screen background or border; Bright School desktop and mobile overrides explicitly keep the pending panel, its content wrapper, and the countdown row transparent so generic paper-surface guards cannot reintroduce a white card behind the timer. Ready recruitment keeps only the centered 瞧瞧有没有新部员！ action, mobile item buttons may collapse to icon plus quantity only, and unavailable recruitment confirmation renders as a gray native disabled button. No-response states can layer the cropped /assets/recruitment/recruitment-envelope-flat.webp sticker, success can layer /assets/recruitment/celebration-flat-candidate.webp, opening recruitment plays /assets/music/recruitment-open.ogg, and claimed recruitment results play one SFX through the existing sfx channel: /assets/music/recruitment-success.ogg on success or /assets/music/recruitment-miss.ogg on no-response.
  • 玩家 UI 当前默认 Bright School 主题；主题注册、CSS 入口和作用域合同保留未来扩展口。共享基础、后台管理、lobby、room、board、modal、commerce、responsive、mobile-room、HUD、mobile-adaptive 等大入口继续保持 import-only 并委托到对应子目录，低频后台与剧情教学对弈 CSS 由 lazy 路由导入。Bright School 根入口现在只保留 base.css、gallery-polish.css、surface-contracts.css、component-repairs.css 和 qa-guard.css 的后置结构：surface-contracts/ 承接仍必要的显式 HUD 去污染与语义表面合同，已删除旧广域兜底层和空兼容结构；component-repairs/ 恢复有意的纸张/手账细节；quality-base/、commerce/、home/、room/、modals/、mobile/ 和 effects/ 继续由 qa-guard.css 后置挂载，最终移动端安全层仍在 themes.css 之后执行。

  • 匹配成功后先保留 3 秒“匹配成功”倒计时弹窗；倒计时结束后才进入对局资源预加载页，该页复用登录加载页视觉，只显示本机玩家出战角色的跳动立绘和角色加载台词，并显示 资源加载中 0/2 到 2/2 的服务端 ready 人数，不再显示转圈动画。前端只有在匹配成功倒计时完成且房间已离开 preloading（双方资源 ready）后才进入对局页；若倒计时先结束则进入并停留在资源加载页，若资源先完成则继续显示倒计时并在倒计时结束后直接进入对局页。刷新或重连恢复到仍处于 preloading 的玩家房间时，客户端会重新构造已完成倒计时的 pending match 并进入 match-preloading，重新加载本地对局资源并发送 room:preload-ready，而不是把该快照直接写入 active room。登录启动预加载以当前账号可访问的非棋谱图片、音乐和音声作为阻塞清单，使用 6 路并发与单资源超时，失败项在进入首页后重试；未购买资源和房间专属对手/Pixi 资源仍延迟到实际对局。客户端 playable-ready 预热会在 home idle、匹配入口意图和开始匹配时提前加载本地模块/Pixi，但仍由 battle preload 和 room:preload-ready 控制可交互时机；房间运行期使用轻量 room:clock、room:patch 与完整 room:update 分流，降低高频交互卡顿；room:clock 与 room:update 共用 clockSeq 时钟序号，前端会忽略比当前房间快照更旧的轻量时钟，避免乱序到达导致棋钟倒转或读秒次数回退。后端房间恢复与活跃房间判定通过用户到房间的内存索引优先定位，避免 room:resume 在房间增多时全量扫描；聊天与在线状态 patch 使用节流持久化，完整房间更新和关键生命周期仍强制持久化。移动端弱网下，启动预加载不订阅瞬时 Socket.IO 实例变化，对局 socket 连接使用明确重连和 6 秒握手超时，避免连接抖动把玩家反复留在资源准备页。
  • Bright School desktop home keeps explicit wide desktop, compact desktop, micro desktop, and mobile portrait layout contracts. In the 1181px-1500px desktop band, the mode stats panel must stay compact and must not cover or visually consume long usernames; the username column owns the remaining width, scales earlier, and clips inside its own tag only as a last resort. Desktop home uses a two-column stage: the left column stacks the player plaque, member handbook, and 3x2 utility toolbox from top to bottom, while the right column is the primary match image button spanning the stack height. Wide and compact desktop keep the stage top spacing and column gaps tight, enlarge the match image as the primary action, and keep match-mode counts in the click-open mode picker instead of rendering tickets under the match entry; the picker buttons keep Chinese/rule/count content above a centered icon-plus-English watermark, using 50% opacity on desktop and 20% opacity in the final mobile layer. Micro desktop keeps a controlled 960px horizontal stage with the same left-stack/right-match hierarchy and low-height desktop windows may scroll vertically. Phone portrait renders the home stage as a single-column usable flow instead of showing an orientation guard; the alpha-trimmed match image row is intentionally taller than the handbook row so the primary match action cannot read smaller than 部员手册. The member handbook, match, and Bright School utility entries are image-only buttons: DOM buttons keep accessible aria-labels, images are decorative, theme layers must not draw data-hud, paper labels, fixed text badges, or the old utility card border/background, and the final narrow-desktop safety layer must preserve clockwise hover/focus rotation instead of replacing it with lift-only motion.
  • Bright School 首页主面板本体现在使用运行期 WebP 背景板资源：桌面和窄桌面由 public/assets/home/home-main-panel-desktop.webp 提供，手机/竖屏由 public/assets/home/home-main-panel-mobile.webp 提供；src/styles/themes/bright-school/home/main-panel-material.css 挂载桌面图并用 background-size: 100% 100% 随面板伸缩，home/mobile-compact.css 与 mobile/home-shell/main-stage.css 切换移动图源。最终 mobile-adaptive/home-narrow-desktop/ 兜底在 compact/micro desktop 仍保留横版木框图的安全内边距，不能退回旧平面卡片的 20px 级内边距；后置 modals/stage-decoration-fixes.css 只清主面板伪元素与内部 .home-stage，不能再把主面板背景图重置为透明。
  • Bright School 首页玩家铭牌使用 GPT Image 2 生成的手绘学生证壳资源：运行时优先引用 public/assets/home/student-id-nameplate.webp，同目录保留 PNG 源图；src/styles/themes/bright-school/home/student-id-card/card-shell-avatar.css 通过 image-set() 把该图挂在 .home-player-plaque.tactical-id-card 的唯一背景层，不再叠加旧粉绿渐变 fallback，并用 background-origin: border-box / background-clip: border-box 让外框、挂孔和分区图铺满整张卡片而不是被 padding 挤进内容区。图片只提供外框、左头像安全区、中部用户名留白和右侧段位统计留白，不画入用户名、头像、段位、积分或 STUDENT ID 文案；这些动态内容继续由 React DOM、UserIdentity、.plaque-stats 和可靠 CSS 文案渲染。按钮本体、头像容器与段位面板只保留尺寸、间距和文本布局，不再绘制旧背景、边框或 box-shadow 外框，避免和生成图里的学生证分区重复；.plaque-stats 通过 --home-student-id-stats-center-x/y、--home-student-id-stats-width/height 绝对定位到右侧纸页安全区中心，默认桌面中心为 88% / 52%、宽高为 clamp(104px, 24%, 126px) / 70px，最终移动/窄桌面层使用同一右页中心并把宽高收束为 clamp(82px, 24%, 96px) / 60px；铭牌 grid 第三列必须直接引用 var(--home-student-id-stats-width)，避免绝对定位的统计层仍通过过宽固定列挤压中间用户名列。内部三列居中展示收束后的模式图标和 WuWa 数字，段位数字为红色，级位数字为深紫色，视觉层不渲染“段/级”文字；装备用户名背景时，首页铭牌的 .user-identity-name 必须使用共享 --user-nameplate-font-size，让文字、背景和安全 padding 一起缩放，不能继承外层移动端大字号导致 8 个英文字符被裁成半截；铭牌按钮本体只保留与部员手册、匹配入口图一致的较近深色 drop-shadow(5px 6px 0 rgba(61, 43, 37, 0.3)) 和 hover/focus 小角度旋转，头像与段位内层仍保持透明且无边框阴影；头像容器通过共享的画框中心和尺寸变量锚定到生成图左侧立绘画框，容器内部立绘继续用 grid/contain 水平垂直居中；旧纸夹伪元素保持关闭，后置 modals.css / mobile.css / mobile-adaptive.css 不得重新覆盖铭牌背景，最终窄桌面和 portrait 安全层会重复透明外壳、头像居中和段位统计定位契约；该契约同时适配桌面和移动端的现有三列布局。
  • Bright School 铭牌段位统计层虽然视觉上位于右页安全区，但 CSS 绝对定位基准必须跨整张 .home-player-plaque.tactical-id-card，不能重新挂到第三个 grid column；否则 left 百分比会按窄列计算并把第三个模式推到右边界外。
  • Bright School 移动端玩家铭牌的模式图标和数字比桌面合同小 20%：mobile/home-shell/player-plaque.css、home/mobile-compact.css 与最终 mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/home-player-plaque.css 覆盖 .plaque-mode-icon 为 clamp(19.2px, 17.6cqw, 24px)，覆盖 .plaque-mode-rank 为 clamp(14.4px, 13.6cqw, 19.2px)；桌面和窄桌面继续保留 clamp(24px, 22cqw, 30px) / clamp(18px, 17cqw, 24px)，避免移动端修正反向改变中桌面铭牌比例。
  • Bright School home utility buttons use user-drawn horizontal label assets in public/assets/home/home-utility-*.webp, converted as compact high-quality WebP files from the 1500x600 PNG drawings in C:/codex/image/main/ with the original artwork proportions and alpha shape preserved; PNG source copies are kept beside the runtime WebP files. The recruitment entry now uses C:/codex/image/main/Recruit_btn1.png, mirrored as public/assets/home/home-utility-recruitment.png and encoded at the highest quality that remains below the runtime WebP size gate. HomeUtilityDock still renders native buttons with aria-labels and DOM icon/title fallbacks for non-Bright themes, but Bright School hides those fallback nodes and displays .utility-entry-art only. Utility entries reuse the member-handbook/match image treatment: transparent button chrome, a single closer dark drop-shadow(5px 6px 0 rgba(61, 43, 37, 0.3)) on the image art, desktop hover/focus rotation, active compressed image shadow, and reduced-motion transform cancellation. The six desktop entries keep their native button containers untransformed and give .utility-entry-motion deterministic clockwise/counterclockwise --utility-tilt transforms; .utility-entry-art keeps filter/shadow ownership only. Each tone also defines a same-direction --utility-hover-tilt around 3.8deg to 4.4deg, and hover/focus adds a shared scale(1.015) compensation so reverse-tilted long labels do not appear to shrink by crossing zero. Fixed grid-auto-rows keep the main-panel/background height and hit boxes stable. Recruitment-ready adds only a subtle pink art glow instead of redrawing the old card background. The user-drawn canvases keep more transparent side bearing than the legacy generated buttons, so Bright School keeps .utility-entry-motion at 121% width for the mobile baseline and raises only the desktop motion wrapper to 128% while the nested image fills that wrapper without stretching the raster content; the desktop grid gap contracts to clamp(4px, 0.55vw, 8px) so the 3x2 toolbox reads denser. Desktop utility entries additionally use restrained tone-scoped --utility-x, --utility-y, and --utility-tilt offsets so the 3x2 toolbox reads like casually placed stickers; these offsets remain on the motion wrapper through hover and active states rather than moving the button/grid item or filtered raster. On phone and tablet widths, src/styles/mobile-adaptive/home-utility-interactions.css keeps the .utility-image-entry button container untransformed, borderless, and paddingless for hover, focus, and active states so the hand-drawn button background cannot stretch; mobile feedback splits lift/press transforms onto .utility-entry-motion and brightness/shadow filters onto .utility-entry-art. Phone portrait and final narrow-desktop safety layers keep the same image-only 2x3 toolbox contract and must not restore the old icon-plus-title columns; the portrait stage gap stays tight, the match row uses clamp(176px, 52vw, 224px) while the handbook row uses clamp(148px, 42vw, 188px), and utility buttons keep a 52px-plus minimum target so the toolbox remains inside the mobile main-panel frame.
  • Desktop home interaction keeps a zero-visual-change compositor contract. AppRoutes wraps the home surface in memoized HomeRoute, preventing unrelated overlay/room state from rerendering HomeScreen when its actual inputs are stable; this thin boundary preserves HomeScreen's onLogout / onSelectCharacter / onStartMatch / onStartPractice prop names end to end, with DOM regression coverage for every mode and practice startup so memoization cannot silently drop actions. 部员手册 and 星炬对弈 use .home-entry-motion around the filtered raster, while the six utility entries use .utility-entry-motion around .utility-entry-art; transforms/will-change belong to the wrapper and existing drop-shadow/brightness/saturation values stay on the nested image. Startup critical image preload waits for browser decode() after load when available and falls back safely when unavailable or rejected; the same login progress also completes accessible shop/warehouse/recruitment/owned-character/decoration images, default or owned BGM, and owned-character skill/system voices. These changes do not bake shadows into assets or change current geometry, so future home image replacement remains a URL/file and transparent-canvas/aspect-ratio operation.

维护约定

  • 入口文档只写总览、导航和跨主题规则；详细事实写入对应分篇。
  • 分篇标题、说明和新增内容优先使用中文；代码标识、路径、事件名、模型名保留原文。
  • 发现乱码时先确认源文件是否含 Unicode replacement character 或常见 mojibake 片段，再区分终端显示问题和文件内容损坏。
  • 修改 Markdown 后运行 npm run docs:system-design；涉及脚本或编码规则时运行 npm test -- docs/systemDesignHtml.test.js。

Admin Deployment Defaults

  • Non-user admin configuration is captured in server/adminDefaultSnapshot.js, generated from local prisma/dev.db by npm run admin:snapshot. npm run check:admin-snapshot compares the ignored local database with the committed snapshot and makes npm run check fail when system settings, recruitment copy, skill traits, characters/skills, catalogs, achievements, music labels, stories/tutorials, or announcements were edited locally but not exported; internal migration.* markers are excluded. Ordinary startup still runs create-only seedAdminDefaultConfig() after schema guards and before built-in seeders, preserving existing rows across restarts. Formal deployment is intentionally different: after the verified SQLite backup and migrations, deploy/update-production.sh previews and applies npm run admin:sync-defaults, updating matching snapshot rows and creating missing rows while preserving cloud-only rows and all user-owned/history/runtime domains. Users, user assets, purchases, draw history, feedback, reports, audit logs, analytics, announcement read-state, mailbox batches/messages, game records, and live-room state remain excluded. A scoped npm run admin:snapshot:onboarding / admin:sync-onboarding path remains available for exceptional one-story maintenance, but normal releases use the full non-user snapshot so local admin state reaches the cloud predictably.

Board Effect Theme Guard

  • Mobile match-mode picker cards keep rule text split into two stable lines, pin the count/status chip to the far right edge of each mode button, keep the status label on one line, and shrink the centered decorative icon-plus-English watermark so it remains behind the Chinese/rule/count content without creating horizontal scroll.
  • Room chat and presence changes now use lightweight room:patch paths with eventId, baseRevision, revision, and client-side gap recovery through room:resume; patch handlers skip missing-room, wrong-room, unknown, stale, and malformed payloads before scheduling React state work; chat:append appends messages idempotently, while presence:update updates player connection flags, spectators, and system chat without replacing game, structurally shares unchanged player/spectator/chat entries, and recovered live room:resume plus pending match-success snapshots reuse the same full-snapshot structural sharing path as room:update; duplicate pending match-success snapshots and unchanged pending-match clocks are consumed without scheduling fresh match-transition state. Board handlers refresh through a board-shell rerender when needed, stay stable across clock churn through useRoomPointActions and stable RoomBattleStage callbacks, stale room:clock payloads are ignored before scheduling React state writes, duplicate lobby:stats / match:waiting / duel:incoming payloads keep their current app-shell state objects stable and do not replay direct-duel doorbell audio, chat/member/action-hint/action-bar widgets ignore clock-only player time changes where their rendered inputs are unchanged, finished-room close countdown ticks stay local to RoomHeader, timed request toasts depend on request-state keys instead of whole-room clock churn, and point buttons stay memoized; startup preload tests guard current-user-accessible assets so visible/owned home, shop, inventory, character, BGM, and voice resources block first entry while timed-out resources retry after entry.
  • App audio runtime state now lives in src/app/useAudioRuntimeState.js, keeping local audio settings persistence and socket-reconnect resume signaling out of the src/app/App.jsx composition root.
  • 首页铭牌（履历）、仓库、观战、好友和排行入口分别使用 ui_resume_open.ogg、ui_warehouse_open.ogg、ui_watch_open.ogg、ui_friends_open.ogg、ui_leaderboard_open.ogg 专属点击音效；它们复用统一 sfx 音量/静音与预加载通道，并通过 data-ui-sound="none" 避免叠放通用确认音。
  • 首页 BGM 现在由 MUSIC_TRACKS 的 home 曲目池解析；默认包含 home-default 的 /assets/music/main_bgm.ogg 与 home-main-bgm-1 的 /assets/music/main_bgm_1_once.ogg + /assets/music/main_bgm_1_loop.ogg，用户每次进入 home 视图时随机抽取一首可用主界面 BGM，并在本次停留期间保持稳定。
  • Admin console styles now follow the import-only CSS domain pattern through src/styles/admin.css and src/styles/admin/, so character, audit, gacha, achievement, and responsive rules no longer live in the root stylesheet entry. src/styles/admin/polish.css is the final desktop admin control skin, keeping buttons, inputs, tables, tabs, danger actions, and close controls light, readable, and consistent after feature-specific admin CSS; its polish/hud-isolation.css child owns the high-specificity resets that keep admin headings, setting containers, and form controls out of Startorch/HUD skins.

  • 普通实时对局、观战和棋谱回放不再渲染聊天按钮、聊天 tab、聊天面板或输入框，客户端房间路由也不再绑定 chat:send；服务端聊天事件和已有房间聊天数据暂时保留兼容。移动房间恢复固定 100dvh 与紧凑“操作 / 成员”dock，桌面端操作提示移到右侧自己玩家面板下方，接管原聊天控件位置。成员行的操作按钮区以及后续用户详情/确认层通过 portal 挂到最近的 .app-shell，避免被移动 dock 的滚动和裁切规则隐藏，同时保留 Bright School 主题祖先与房间浮层层级。剧情教学是唯一例外：通过 showTutorialLog 在桌面和移动端都显示可开关、不可发送的“剧情记录”弹窗，继续承载 NPC 和选项回复历史；移动端入口占满“操作”右侧的等分标签区域，记录内容会从该按钮上方向上弹出，通过 portal 逃离 dock 裁切并按视口收边，同时明确排除通用紧凑布局的聊天隐藏规则，不再压缩成 dock 内的两三行记录或被隐藏。
  • 教学对弈退出确认使用“结束教学”作为确认动作。当前步骤显示黄色目标圈时，落子或技能选点偏离目标统一提示“请落子或选择黄圈位置”；没有目标圈的落子题继续保留脚本自定义反馈。无目标圈落子进入 NPC 错误分支后，错误台词完整显示，再固定停留 1.5 秒自动推进或复原，不要求手动继续，也不受旧脚本中该反馈节点的继续模式影响。
  • 移动端真实对局、观战和棋谱回放继续共用 RoomScreen / RoomBattleStage。useRoomBoardView 通过 roomViewStatusFor() 统一输出控制模式、实时跟随状态和黑/白视角，但房间 header 不再重复显示“实时观战 / 观战回看 / 棋谱回放 · X方视角”小字；视角切换仍只由玩家头像真实按钮承担。棋谱回放和观战回看共用七槽 ReplayActionBar：首手、后退五手、上一手、居中的纯文本手数、下一手、前进五手、末手，五手跳转在 0..replayMax 内截断；末手按钮只显示图标，观战回看仍通过 title / aria-label 暴露“回到实时”语义。移动玩家条中的合法最大用户名保持单行完整，历史超长用户名允许换行但不省略，并继续保留称号、徽章和用户名背景装备。
  • 观战“对局列表”的星炬、标准和五子棋选项卡在桌面与移动端都以三列等宽铺满整行；按钮文字与履历模式选项卡一致为 16px，模式标题与房间数之间保留 1em 间距。GET /api/rooms/watch?mode=<mode> 从一次公开可观战房间快照返回当前模式 rooms 和全模式 roomCounts，按钮在模式名旁显示对应数量，切换模式或手动刷新时同步更新。练习房由公开观战读模型排除，因此不会进入列表或数量。棋谱分页到末尾时保持静默，不再显示“已加载全部棋谱”，加载、空态和错误反馈不变。

  • Admin-managed character sortOrder is persistent data, not a seed-owned builtin field. seedCharacters only assigns fallback order when creating missing builtin characters, public /api/characters includes sortOrder, and the member manual derives its character list through the shared catalog sorter so saved admin order survives server restarts and reaches player UI.
  • ChangLi (changli) uses the double-move active skill. The server persists the public chained-action state in game.extraTurn; board confirmation is not tied to an intersection, and restoring during ChangLi skill preview resolves directly into the double-move state without replaying the banner, sound, or animation.
  • Lynae (lynae) spray-stone records its resolved randomTargetId in skill history. Replay reconstruction must pass that recorded target back into the shared spray resolver instead of rerolling, so replay step changes cannot turn different historical stones into spray stones.
  • Hidden-hand is an active skill history entry with effectType: "hidden-hand", so ChangLi unlock checks treat Aemeath's resolved hidden-hand the same as other opponent active skills while still ignoring passive effects. Aemeath's board presentation now replaces the older data sweep with a 1500ms green Pixi circuit-board takeover from board center to edge, including only line-based circuit lanes, sparse diagonal links, a light outline membrane, and procedural low-startup/high-data/takeover-pulse SFX while avoiding any target-point focus, center green ring, or final full-board darkening pass. Bright School's late skill-action layer also owns a disabled override for .action-bar .skill-action:disabled, keeping unavailable skills gray instead of inheriting active/normal gradients.
  • Aemeath hidden-hand now creates a public game.derivedSkills[color] slot for the derived active skill voyage-star / “远航星” after the hidden hand resolves. Owner views keep sourceHiddenHandId; opponent/spectator views redact that source id through gameViewForColor while still showing the derived skill name and remaining use count. voyage-star is a no-target board-surface confirmation skill with one derived use, default numeric overclock 5, no turn consumption, and availability gated by the source hidden hand still existing, belonging to the caster, and remaining unexposed. Its shared rules erase the source cross, remove stones around those erased points, clear ko, resolve zero-liberty cleanup as skill removals, persist star-crater invalid points with skillEffect: "voyage-star-erased-point", and keep replay deterministic from history metadata.
  • Voyage Star's board presentation stores the source hidden-hand center as voyage-star-crater-point and the remaining cross intersections as voyage-star-erased-point; only one crater marker renders as a board-level overlay whose left/top are derived from the source history id's board coordinates. The marker uses the supplied heart/star artwork converted to /assets/effects/voyage-star-crater.webp, is sized to 3.5 board-cell edge lengths, and continuously emits tight pink-purple/cyan drop-shadow glow that follows the WebP alpha shape instead of a round haze. The marker is not a child of the erased .point, so its center is independent from point-child layout, stone offsets, and theme point repairs. Pixi board effects resolve point centers from the rendered .point[data-point-id] DOM rectangles first, falling back to formula coordinates only when the DOM point is unavailable, so desktop and mobile themes share the same visual coordinate source as stones and grid cells. The Pixi cast preloads the crater WebP but does not draw a temporary crater: it begins with a subtle white-gold omen and vertical beams, drops a white/ice-blue knight greatsword from above the board with a short trail, then keeps the sword visible through impact while a quakeLayer applies visual-only shake, concentric earthquake ellipses, and deterministic dust sparks. Its yellow-white explosion cover is rendered above the sword, impact glow, and removal particles, expands from the skill center with a solid warm-white core whose radius is at least 2.2 board cells so the roughly four-cell crater area stays masked, then blends through low-alpha white-to-warm-yellow discs whose outer feather fades to zero alpha. It switches to a fully opaque white full-board cover once the radius reaches the board corners so neither the board nor the resolved crater can show through during the full-cover stage, holds that opaque cover for resolution, then dissolves it with easing plus outward yellow-white rings, radial streaks, and spark fragments instead of hard-cutting from pure white back to the board. The impact glow also fades before full-cover, and the sword is forced fully transparent as soon as that full-board cover is reached. The server resolves voyage-star during this full-cover window, and BoardSkillEffects keeps the already-started Pixi cast alive after pendingSkill clears so the cover can hide the resolved DOM crater and removed stones until the effect ends. This prevents the crater from showing through too early, avoids a sudden post-effect crater pop, and prevents a Pixi crater plus DOM crater double-flash. It does not stack multiple craters or also render Sigrika's erased-field marker. The action bar reads the effective self skill from the current room player snapshot on both desktop and mobile, keeping the button label in sync when Aemeath's derived skill becomes active. Replay reconstruction consumes the voyage-star history metadata directly so mobile and desktop replay boards show the erased cross even when the live derived-skill slot is no longer available.
  • Board latest-action marking treats Aemeath hidden-hand and voyage-star as point actions, alongside ordinary moves and flip-stone. This moves the red latest-action ring onto the hidden-hand placement when 小爱出击 resolves and prevents the ring from remaining on the previous ordinary move after 远航星 erases its source point, avoiding false visual evidence that the derived skill was centered elsewhere.
  • Bright School board guards keep DOM-only board effect layers transparent and restore their pseudo-elements so legacy broad surface cleanup cannot turn QiuYuan's slash overlay into a blank paper panel or a plain bar over the board; the row-slash owner rule also keeps .board-row-slash at max-width: none !important so the portrait mobile all-element max-width: 100% !important safety layer cannot cap the scar back to board width. Mornye's protocol marker remains a pointer-events-none point child and never changes board hit testing. The playable board surface is shared through --board-wood-texture, currently /assets/boards/go-board-background-reference-color-vertical-2048.webp, a low-saturation vertical bamboo/wood WebP asset reused by the base room CSS and Bright School guard layers.
  • Board move preview and mobile touch-confirmation markers are point-local visual effects centered by the .point absolute coordinate contract, so ChangLi's extra-turn ordinary placement hints stay on the same intersections as stones and SVG grid lines. Touch confirmation uses only the inner .touch-confirm-marker; .point.touch-confirming::before stays hidden, and mobile point press/confirmation states preserve translate(-50%, -50%) even when adding scale or reduced-motion overrides, preventing duplicate blue rings, apparent marker drift, and stones animating from grid cells back to intersections after placement.
  • Default black/white board stones are ringless: shared stone CSS and Bright School default overrides avoid border and inset shadow outlines, while semantic skill states can still draw glow/state halos.
  • Chisa's liberty-purge now has a post-banner targeted Pixi cast: Board first shows a delayed pending placement on the target point using that player's selected stone decoration, then BoardSkillEffects reads removalMarkIds and draws several saturated red scissor slashes in sequence only over the points that will receive removal marks, excluding the newly placed stone. The server derives the board-effect window from removalMarkIds.length, so rooms with many removals keep the pending preview open until every removal slash has had time to play; the persistent skill result appears immediately after the slashes fade. A resolved Chisa placement that remains on the board carries the persistent liberty-purge-stone eerie red glow instead of reusing the latest-move ring, while resolved .liberty-purge-removal-mark crosses remain point-local DOM effects centered with left/top: 50% and translate(-50%, -50%).

Modal Layering Note

  • Browser request toasts render through a document.body portal, keeping them outside .mobile-room-screen so mobile board sizing and position do not shift when draw, counting, or result-confirmation request toasts appear or disappear.
  • Chisa (chisa) uses the liberty-purge active skill. The server resolves a normal legal move first, then removes all one-liberty groups from a board snapshot, records removalMarkIds, clamps negative overclock deltas to zero, clears ko, and keeps .liberty-purge-removal-mark red crosses until the opponent's next real turn ends. While those red crosses are active, the marked clearAfterColor treats the same empty intersections as forbidden points for ordinary placement and legal-move skill targets, so desktop and mobile previews/click confirmation must read the shared libertyPurgeMarks helper instead of relying only on point-local data. Because liberty-purge places a real stone, the placed point records skillEffect: "liberty-purge-stone" / skillEffectOwner for its persistent red glow when it survives the purge, while lastMarkedAction does not add the generic latest-move ring to that skill placement. Admin users own Chisa by default until recruitment is implemented.
  • Shared .modal-backdrop stacks above room --room-floating-z surfaces so request and confirmation modals dim skill chips, chat controls, and room member popovers together. RoomBattleStage uses a bounded two-level room stack: ordinary fallbacks top out at 140, and only the latest interacted surface receives 141, so first-hover skill details beat untouched chat/member controls without repeated interactions ever climbing past the modal backdrop at 160. 房间内申请和棋、申请数子与确认数子结果的定时 toast 由流程响应、阶段变化或自动消失关闭，不提供手动关闭按钮。
  • Character duplicate chain counts remain in user data for reward/progression logic, but player-facing desktop and mobile UI hides character-chain badges on portrait surfaces.
  • Current built-in character, costume, and Denia candy-effect portraits use normalized WebP assets under /assets/characters/portraits/ and /assets/costumes/portraits/. npm run portraits:normalize alpha-trims each configured local asset, proportionally fits the complete visible subject into a shared 792px safe box, and bottom-centers it on a transparent 900×900 per-frame canvas. Static inputs remain lossless; animated inputs use the union of all frame bounds, retain their frame count, delays and loop metadata, and apply one shared transform to every frame. The Denia candy catalog entry explicitly requires animation, so npm run check:portraits rejects a static-first-frame regression in addition to missing, opaque, non-WebP, oversized, off-anchor, or non-normalized repository portraits. Remote HTTP(S) portraits remain supported but are reported as skipped without network access. Legacy portrait files stay at their old paths so saved room/replay snapshots retain their original URL and framing; startup only migrates exact untouched built-in character/costume catalog rows to the new paths and resets those costume rows to 100/0/0, preserving admin-customized rows and all user/history data.
  • The login title uses a dedicated /assets/login-sigrika-mascot.webp image instead of reusing CHARACTERS.sigrika.portrait, so replacing the title mascot cannot change 西格莉卡 portraits in character lists, loading screens, profiles, or battles. Its retained PNG source and runtime WebP use a transparent 640x640 canvas; the artwork is cropped only at transparent edges, uniformly scaled, and centered without non-uniform stretching. AuthScreen applies the semantic .text-window-title hook so the Chinese club title resolves to the existing 霞鹜漫黑 window-title font. The mascot is an absolute, pointer-transparent decoration hanging over the card's top-left corner: desktop uses an equal 240x240 box with a -6deg rotation and negative top/left offsets while preserving the original title column; the final bright-school-overrides/auth-login-lockup.css owner gives portrait phone equal clamp(140px, 39vw, 148px) dimensions and a viewport-safe left offset so the title remains single-line without horizontal page overflow. The form keeps its default state terse: registration shows only the username-width and 8-64 位 label notes, while concrete causes appear beside invalid fields. Mode changes retain the username but clear passwords; password visibility controls remain 44px; submission uses a synchronous lock, abortable request, pending state, first-invalid-field focus, and an accessible in-app session-conflict dialog instead of window.confirm.
  • Character-detail dialogs expose the active character palette as a local theme token: ordinary and derived skill-name labels use a light mix of that character color with black text, while the acquisition label uses a neutral gray background with a square-corner border. Mobile character-detail dialogs keep the character name horizontal and single-line, place the optional CV label below the name, place the compact BGM preview capsule at the top-right of the heading row, left-align skill and character-description copy, and omit the standalone skill-cost badge; Bright School theme layers and the final mobile-adaptive safety layer repeat these constraints so narrow phones cannot fall back to vertical names or centered body text.

Music Management Note

  • 莫宁（mornye）已接入完整角色语音：技能、出战、对局开始、读秒开始、剩余读秒次数、10 到 1 秒倒计时、胜负和结果，以及 house-detail 角色详情语音。部员手册中打开莫宁角色详情或点击详情内的“描述”文本区域会通过现有 house-detail 事件播放 /assets/voice/mornye_detail.ogg；关闭角色详情弹窗或关闭整个部员手册时会调用 stopVoicePlayback() 停止当前角色语音，避免详情长语音在窗口关闭后继续播放。
  • 新用户或没有有效本地音频设置的环境默认使用 master: 100、bgm: 60、sfx: 100、voice: 100；已有 sigrika-audio-settings 继续覆盖默认值。角色音频和 TTS 不再因播放状态衰减 BGM，背景音乐始终保持用户设置音量；requestBackgroundMusicDuck() 只保留给招募演出等明确的非语音场景。10 到 1 秒倒计时解码语音仍绕过空灵混响，但继续使用 voice 音量、增益和 RMS 响度归一化。
  • 角色技能 BGM 当前配置：主界面默认曲池包含 home-default（main_bgm.ogg）和 home-main-bgm-1（main_bgm_1_once.ogg + main_bgm_1_loop.ogg），达妮娅使用 canonical denia-skill-default（bgm_*），西格莉卡使用 sigrika_intro_once.ogg + sigrika_loop.ogg，爱弥斯“小爱出击”使用 aemeath0_once.ogg + aemeath0_loop.ogg，爱弥斯“远航星”使用 lhl_intro_once.ogg + lhl_loop.ogg，猪小仙使用 matoya_*，娜波摩使用 busizhe_*，千咲使用 chisa_intro_once.ogg + chisa_loop.ogg，长离使用 changli_intro_once.ogg + changli_loop.ogg，莫宁使用 mornye_intro_once.ogg + mornye_loop.ogg。旧达妮娅 slug 会在启动清理阶段迁移/删除，运行时 BGM resolver 只按 canonical denia 查找达妮娅技能 BGM；pending skill 与最新历史技能使用独立 musicEffectType 选择普通或派生槽位，派生技能的 musicTrackId 同时作为槽位 fallback 延续，避免演出中途回退到角色普通技能 BGM。未来爱弥斯商城技能 BGM 若替换“小爱出击”默认曲，继续写入 musicSelections.skill.aemeath；若替换“远航星”等派生技默认曲，则写入现有 musicSelections.derivedSkill.aemeath[effectType] 槽位。
  • Settings > Audio persists optional per-channel mute flags under audioSettings.muted; clicking an audio row title toggles mute without changing the slider percentage, and moving the slider clears that channel's mute flag.
  • Settings > Interface presents three player-facing theme choices in a three-column grid on desktop and mobile. Theme option labels are the direct style names without a repeated 主题： prefix or explanatory parenthetical. Only the first option maps to the current Bright School theme; the two future theme buttons keep native button feedback and show the non-blocking 敬请期待~ toast without changing persisted theme state. On portrait mobile, the Interface tab gets its own taller settings-tab-theme modal state so the three theme buttons fit without an inner vertical scrollbar.

Dependency Notes

  • roughjs is installed as a runtime drawing dependency for Bright School hand-drawn SVG/Canvas decoration, such as notebook outlines, stickers, circles, and annotation strokes. Character detail BGM previews import it for the stable campus notebook sketch layer.
  • exceljs is installed as a browser-side admin workbook dependency for剧情教学脚本 .xlsx export/import. The library is isolated behind src/admin/storyScriptWorkbook.js so React components do not depend on workbook APIs directly.
  • Tailwind CSS v4 is installed as a build-time utility CSS dependency through @tailwindcss/vite. The app imports src/styles/tailwind.css after hud-components.css and before the final theme layer; that entry imports Tailwind theme.css, project semantic tokens from src/styles/tailwind/tokens.css, and Tailwind utilities.css with the tw: prefix and source("../"), while omitting preflight/global resets so existing Bright School CSS contracts are not reset. Tailwind migration is now a staged Phase 1-7 route, with the durable full execution plan in .trellis/tasks/07-01-tailwind-migration-roadmap/tailwind-full-migration-plan.md: Phase 1 only records contracts and token scaffolding, Phase 2 pilots isolated admin/tooling surfaces, Phase 3 establishes UI primitives, Phases 4-7 migrate shared UI, player non-gameplay flows, Bright School tokens, and mobile safety layers in order, while Phase 8+ protected gameplay/skill work requires visual regression gates before starting. The first Phase 2 pilot is the admin-only audit table shell in src/admin/AdminAudit.jsx, which now consumes AdminTableScroll backed by src/ui/primitives/ScrollArea.jsx after removing the old .audit-table-wrap audit CSS; Phase 3 now has ScrollArea for overflow utilities, AdminTableScroll for all admin table shells, Badge for low-risk inline badge layout utilities, EmptyState for admin table empty-cell alignment/spacing through AdminTableEmpty, and Button for admin action alignment through AdminActionButton, while existing CSS still owns visual treatments such as colors, borders, spacing contracts, disabled states, shadows, and typography. Phase 4 has started with the narrow ModalActionButton wrapper in src/modals/modalComponents.jsx; ConfirmModal consumes it for danger/secondary actions, MessageBoardModal consumes it for the submit action, AnnouncementModal consumes it for simple retry/load-more secondary actions, PersonalizationModal consumes it for the save action, MailboxModal consumes it for the attachment claim action, FriendsOverlays consumes it for the duel-mode cancel action, and UserProfileCard consumes it for the profile report submit action while shared modal/action, mailbox, and friends/profile CSS still owns visual styling, list/detail layout, paper background, match-mode options, profile confirm panels, profile social actions, and mobile treatment. Room gameplay, board geometry, Pixi canvas hosts, skill-presentation marks/keyframes, player-facing button restyling, and final mobile gameplay controls remain CSS-entry owned until a later protected-surface migration has visual regression coverage.
  • Phase 5 has started with the narrow HomeActionButton wrapper in src/home/homeComponents.jsx; MatchModePicker in src/home/HomeScreen.jsx uses it only for the match-mode cancel action, preserving the existing .secondary-action visual class while Button contributes the low-risk tw: alignment utilities. Home/modal/mobile CSS still owns match-mode layout, mode option buttons, spacing, artboard behavior, decorative imagery, colors, borders, shadows, typography, and responsive safety until a full home/lobby/commerce surface has focused desktop/mobile tests and visual checks.
  • npm test excludes local Codex scratch folders such as .codex-run/ in addition to worktrees, e2e tests, and stability tests, so temporary staged snapshots cannot be collected as Vitest suites during handoff.
  • Impeccable design review automation is available as a project-scoped skill in .agents/skills/impeccable: .impeccable/config.json enables the detector hook, .codex/hooks.json preserves the Trellis prompt hook and adds Impeccable PostToolUse, PRODUCT.md records the product design register, and .impeccable/live/config.json points live mode at the Vite index.html shell. Codex 0.142 parses .codex/hooks.json with a strict schema, so the shared manifest keeps only the top-level hooks object and stores descriptive notes in system-design docs instead of the JSON file.
  • Player theme metadata now has one source of truth in src/app/visualTheme.js: VISUAL_THEME_OPTIONS includes both available and future settings choices, while VISUAL_THEMES / VISUAL_THEME_IDS expose only enabled themes that can be persisted and imported. Settings renders from the full option registry, so future styles can be listed without duplicating labels in the modal. CSS refactors must preserve the root theme order shared -> isolation -> theme-components -> bright-school -> mobile-adaptive; board, Pixi skill overlays, point buttons, and Bright School effect files remain protected skill-presentation surfaces and should not be broad-selector cleanup targets.
  • CSS round-2 inventory lives in src/styles/cssLayerInventory.js with tests in src/styles/cssLayerInventory.test.js. The inventory separates reorganizable shared domains from high-risk gameplay/room CSS, Bright School-specific overrides, final mobile safety layers, and skill-presentation protected files so later cleanup can stay scoped before visual and skill regression verification. Character detail music styling is registered as two import-only splits: shared shell/title, track-sheet, and motion files under modals/character-music-player/, plus Bright School shell and track-sheet skin files under component-repairs/character-music-player/. The closed player title deliberately contains only the current music name; ordinary/derived identity stays in the open sheet tabs. Bright School keeps the closed shell transparent, uses a single CSS-owned rounded-square hardware key with normal UI typography, and limits Rough.js to one quiet title underline instead of wrapping the control in a second decorative frame. The outer 44px hit target stays transparent across all interaction states; only the inset 32px key face changes from transparent to green on hover/focus and red while pressed. The active state models a 2px physical press by shortening the bottom shadow instead of scaling the control. Character-detail nested overlays use a static theme-owned translucent ::before paint layer; both it and .nested-modal-backdrop explicitly disable backdrop filtering so Chrome never maintains a full-screen live blur during descendant interaction.
  • CSS scalability cleanup now records all-src/styles debt and expansion contracts in CSS_DEBT_BASELINE, CSS_Z_INDEX_CONTRACT, CSS_MOTION_CONTRACT, and CSS_BREAKPOINT_CONTRACT. The current baseline includes the admin story node settings window CSS, story workbook import feedback, plus the frontend layout-polish contracts for hidden player-window scrollbars, mobile music shop cards, left-aligned replay times, story-player parent padding, mobile announcement badges, Bright School mobile utility-image feedback containment, the dedicated Zahira shop crayon-background owner, the final bright-school-overrides/auth-login-lockup.css owner split, and the exact-asset citrus-sun Semantic Ignition light / wind-tail motion owner; these bounded owner expansions are recorded explicitly instead of polluting shared theme files. The baseline covers file count, CSS bytes, !important, hardcoded hex values, media-query files, reduced-motion files, and high z-index files as a non-growth guardrail rather than a visual target. New z-index values at or above 1000, new motion-heavy families, and new media-query families must be registered or moved onto existing token/layer contracts so desktop and mobile expansion stays auditable.
  • CSS round-3 and round-4 follow-ups are also encoded in src/styles/cssLayerInventory.js: CSS_ROUND3_SHARED_SPLITS records import-only shared-domain splits for base/home-legacy-grid.css, base/home-stage-artboard.css, hud-components/pop-tech-terminal.css, mobile-modals/phone-house-resume.css, modals/character-opening.css, responsive/phone-portrait-room.css, modals/mailbox.css, and commerce/recruitment/board.css; CSS_ROUND4_REGRESSION_CHECKS records the required static CSS, focused board/skill unit, and desktop/mobile Pixi visual stability gates. npm run verify:battle-fixes now includes CSS inventory/theme contracts plus BoardSkillEffects, BoardAmbientEffects, skill registry, skill SFX scheduling, and mobile point-confirmation tests.
  • Full-repo CSS cleanup completion gates are tracked in CSS_FULL_REPO_CLEANUP_VERIFICATION_GATES: each stage runs CSS contracts, high-risk room/board/skill/mobile work runs npm run verify:battle-fixes, protected Pixi/skill presentation work runs npm run verify:stability -- tests/stability/skill-effects.spec.js, and final handoff runs npm run check.
  • The 2026-06-27 full-repo CSS cleanup backlog has been executed on codex/full-repo-css-cleanup; remaining risk is broad page visual coverage because the repo has functional desktop/mobile Playwright stability checks rather than screenshot baselines for every UI surface.
  • CSS tests that assert concrete rules behind import-only entries should expand imports with readCssWithImports(); raw entry reads are reserved for import-order or import-only assertions.
  • Final mobile safety splits are tracked separately as CSS_FINAL_MOBILE_SAFETY_SPLITS because mobile-adaptive.css remains the post-theme override layer. mobile-adaptive/mobile-profile-records.css now delegates to mobile-adaptive/mobile-profile-records/ while preserving the 768px profile/resume record cascade, mobile-adaptive/phone-core.css delegates to mobile-adaptive/phone-core/ for match mode, shell controls, modal tabs, and scroll/detail/result safety, mobile-adaptive/phone-gacha.css delegates to mobile-adaptive/phone-gacha/ for legacy gacha modal, stage, controls, and result dialog survival rules, mobile-adaptive/home-utility-interactions.css owns the final mobile Bright School utility-image button feedback override, Bright School final leaderboard cards delegate to mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/leaderboard-cards/, Bright School portrait resume modal layout/settings tabs delegate to mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/resume-modal-layout/ and mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/settings-tabs/, announcement modal base styling delegates to modals/announcement/, and announcement detail mobile sizing delegates to mobile-adaptive/announcement-detail/.
  • Theme overlay splits are tracked separately as CSS_THEME_OVERLAY_SPLITS. themes/shared.css now delegates to themes/shared/ for player theme tokens, theme settings controls, and player-surface wiring, themes/theme-components.css delegates to themes/theme-components/ for outcome badges, skill chips, and replay row semantic colors, Bright School component repairs such as foundation-home.css, warehouse-character.css, and notebook-polish.css delegate to matching nested directories, Bright School portrait mobile modal-shell.css delegates to mobile/modal-shell/ for shell surfaces and scroll/control rules, and Bright School portrait room shell-header-menu.css, viewport-player-strips.css, and dock-actions.css now delegate to matching directories under themes/bright-school/mobile/room/ while preserving their positions before later Bright School repairs and final mobile safety layers.
  • Gameplay room splits are tracked separately as CSS_GAMEPLAY_ROOM_SPLITS because these selectors affect live room controls. room/actions-requests.css now delegates to room/actions-requests/ for room toggles/action bars, decision/scoring panels, timed request toasts, test tools, replay controls, and disabled action states, mobile-room/portrait-room.css delegates to mobile-room/portrait-room/ for shared portrait viewport, player strip, board/dock, decision, and action-hint rules, and room-terminal/players-timers-skills.css delegates to room-terminal/players-timers-skills/ for the Startorch battlefield player panel, timer, captures, skill chip, detail panel, and sheen keyframe skin while preserving their current import positions before later responsive and theme repair layers.
  • Skill presentation splits are tracked separately as CSS_SKILL_PRESENTATION_SPLITS because these selectors own persistent board point and stone effects. room/board/stones-skill-effects.css now delegates to room/board/stones-skill-effects/ for base stones, hidden/flip/double-move stone states, transient markers, protocol-ban and liberty-purge DOM marks, and related keyframes, while Bright School quality-base/refinement-board.css delegates to quality-base/refinement-board/ for board point, SVG grid, row-slash, and stone-position guard rules; both preserve their import positions before later stone-effect, grid/scoring, and theme repair layers.

Achievement And Personalization Note

  • 用户名注册/搜索校验允许中文、日文、韩文、半角英文、数字和下划线；按显示宽度限制为最多 8 个半角字符宽度，等价于最多 4 个中日韩字符或 8 个半角字符。注册页把用户名、密码和确认密码规范放在输入框 placeholder 中，用户开始输入后自然隐藏；失焦或提交时才在对应字段下方显示错误。登录与注册主提交按钮分别使用“开门！”和“登记入部信息”，密码可见性按钮保持 44px 点击区，但 Bright School 悬停只改变图标色，不再铺整块粉色底。 Legacy startup cleanup trims overlong existing usernames to the trailing 8 display-width units and adds a short numeric marker only when needed to preserve username uniqueness.
  • 成就系统以 Achievement、AchievementRewardAsset、UserAchievement、AchievementCounter 和 UserAchievementEquipment 为核心模型；启动时 ensureAchievementSchema 会先于角色/商店种子任务运行，为旧 SQLite 自动补表，并为 Character 补 source、cvName、cvUrl 字段，为 Decoration 补 source 字段，为 ShopItem 补 source、illustName、illustUrl 字段。玩家侧 /api/achievements 返回合并后的成就列表与本次新解锁成就，/api/me/achievement-equipment 读写称号、徽章和用户名背景装备；成就目标本体由代码/种子维护，后台 /api/admin/achievements 只允许修改成就名、成就内容、奖励资产和排序，/api/admin/achievement-reward-assets 继续管理奖励资产并写审计日志。
  • /api/me、GET/PATCH /api/me/achievement-equipment 会同时返回 achievementEquipment 装备 id 与 achievementEquipmentAssets / equipmentAssets 奖励资产显示数据；socket 登录用户、排行榜和社交用户列表/资料也会批量附加 achievementEquipmentAssets。前端通过共享 UserIdentity 在对弈玩家名、房间成员、排行榜、好友/黑名单、资料卡、观战列表和结算赢家名等完整用户展示点渲染称号、徽章和用户名背景；好友/黑名单列表行只展示状态、常用角色、用户名和操作入口，不再展示段位或积分，详细资料仍保留完整统计。
  • 对局界面的房间成员列表复用 UserIdentity 显示普通用户名、称号和徽章，但显式关闭用户名背景/铭牌，避免桌面端固定成员列为铭牌槽预留宽度后截断合法用户名；对局双方主玩家条以及成员详细资料仍保留完整身份装饰。
  • UserIdentity nameplates are rendered as fixed-ratio username badges: users without a nameplate keep natural-width text, while generic equipped nameplates use a 96px x 25.6px base slot at 3.75:1. The background image and horizontal safe padding scale together through scene-owned --user-nameplate-scale; username text uses shared --user-nameplate-font-size: calc(15px * var(--user-nameplate-scale)) so it scales with the fixed badge while remaining independent from username length, and never emits --user-identity-fit-font-size. The fallback for extreme legacy names is ellipsis rather than per-name font shrinking. The nameplate background wraps only the username, stays centered, and must not return to parent-width stretching or left/center/right DOM slicing. Asset-owned exceptions may provide their own checked delivery ratio when their exact-ID CSS owner uses the matching fixed slot; PNGs should remain alpha-trimmed with scripts/pngTrim.mjs before final resampling.
  • Shared UserIdentity now exposes equipped asset identity through data-nameplate-id and renders explicit background, pointer-transparent effect, and username layers without changing its public props or API payload. Ordinary image nameplates keep the static 96px x 25.6px fallback. The built-in reward-sigrika-spark-100-wins-nameplate is an asset-owned exception in hud-components/user-identity/semantic-ignition-nameplate.css: its 1125 x 240 transparent raster uses a Sigrika-specific hand-painted composition—sun-like Royan rune, dekopon citrus and fresh leaves, deep-plum username carrier, warm starlight, and cyan-violet wind tail—while CSS expands the base slot to 150px x 32px. Its light is illustration-aligned rather than a full-plate energy overlay: an alpha-following gold-violet rim remains continuously visible, the sun disc breathes softly, one small highlight travels across the citrus peel, a bounded warm-white/cyan soft band moves visibly through the central purple carrier, fine edge lines continue into the right tail, and sparse stars twinkle only as a secondary accent. Those animations only change transform and opacity, the effect layer never participates in hit testing, and prefers-reduced-motion keeps the persistent rim plus a clean static illustrated highlight. New bespoke nameplates add their own asset-ID owner; admin-created image-only nameplates require no code and continue using the generic fallback.
  • Because Bright School and portrait/narrow-desktop safety layers load after shared HUD CSS and intentionally use broad !important text resets, bespoke nameplate readability is restored by the exact-asset mobile-adaptive/user-nameplate-final.css owner imported last. It fixes only the Semantic Ignition tag geometry, warm-white username color, deep-purple text shadow, ellipsis fallback, and local effect overflow; generic nameplates and surrounding student-ID layout remain unchanged. The Semantic Ignition raster uses a 1125 x 240 canvas whose uniformly scaled hand-painted subject remains at least 197px tall while leaving at least 8px transparent above/below and 40px left/right. Essential sun, citrus, star, paper, and wind-tail contours therefore stay complete inside the fixed 150 x 32 slot instead of relying on ancestor overflow, while the legal-name safety area remains clear.
  • Repository-local Skill .agents/skills/create-character-nameplate/ standardizes future character nameplate production in new and refine modes. It enforces text-first dossier research: supplied archive pages must be traversed through relevant body sections, tabs, accordions, anchors, pagination, and linked text entries; personality, biography, goals/conflicts, relationships, dialogue, plot development, abilities, meaningful objects/places/hobbies/foods, and achievement relevance are recorded as nine separate, unmerged rows with source locators and found / absent / inaccessible coverage before any visual-language card or concept may be produced. Images are secondary corroboration, and every chosen motif, palette/material decision, and motion verb must trace through textual evidence and interpretation to the visual result; inaccessible character-defining text blocks generation instead of being replaced by portrait guesses. After that gate, the workflow requires four structurally different hand-painted concepts plus an explicit human selection gate for new/major-redesign work, keeps reward/data wiring opt-in, and guides exact asset-ID CSS integration without reusing another role's motifs. Its deterministic validator reports delivered PNG dimensions, RGBA/Alpha bounds, four-edge safety margins, visible-height ratio, and declared username-safe-zone geometry; its task-local Vite preview harness renders the real UserIdentity across representative usernames/scales, while the capture script records 1440x900, 1024x768, and 375x812 in normal and reduced-motion contexts using Playwright Chromium or an installed Chrome/Edge fallback. The preview never enters production routes and does not replace final checks in real identity consumers.
  • 内置成就“百次回响”使用 mode_character_wins 条件统计玩家使用达妮娅在 spark（星炬对弈）模式的胜场，达到 100 胜后解锁 reward-denia-spark-100-wins-nameplate，奖励资源为 /assets/achievements/denia-spark-100-wins-nameplate.png；server/achievements.js 与 server/adminDefaultSnapshot.js 保持相同种子定义，默认管理员快照会标记该内置成就已达成且奖励已发放。选定的 C 版“折光泡泡入口”是 1125 x 240 透明手绘 RGBA：粉色保护泡泡入口为主、相册/照片页碎片为辅，中央粉色载体连续可读，暗紫只负责轮廓、景深和对比，不包含缝补玩偶、布料修补、缝线、角色肖像或烘焙文字。
  • 达妮娅用户名背景只由精确 data-nameplate-id="reward-denia-spark-100-wins-nameplate" 命中。hud-components/user-identity/denia-echo-nameplate.css 将同倍率运行槽设为 150px x 32px，用户名左右安全内边距为 40px / 25px，并维持暖白文字、梅紫阴影、稳定粉紫轮廓光和既有省略回退；denia-echo-nameplate-motion.css 只叠加左侧泡泡入口呼吸、载体内部回响、右侧折光漂移和稀疏回声点，连续关键帧仅改变 transform / opacity，prefers-reduced-motion 下移除运动并保留静态可读轮廓。真实主题级预览覆盖桌面、窄桌面、竖屏手机、亮/暗底、合法英文名、四个中日韩字符、历史超长用户名以及称号＋独立徽章组合，不新增消费端分支或后端特效字段。
