当前音频系统主要由 src/shared/musicLibrary.js、src/shared/audioScheduling.js、src/shared/boardAudio.js、src/shared/voiceEffects.js 与 src/audio/ 下的播放运行时组成。

  • src/audio/audioSettings.js
    • Owns default audio settings, localStorage loading, per-channel volume calculation, and finite-number sanitization for persisted percent values.
    • Audio settings also support optional per-channel mute flags under muted; audioVolume() returns 0 when muted.master or the requested channel flag is true, while keeping the stored 0-100 slider percentage unchanged.
  • src/audio/backgroundDucking.js
    • Owns explicit scoped BGM duck requests and subscribers for authored non-voice scenes such as the recruitment cinematic. Voice and TTS playback do not participate in this registry.
  • src/audio/audioRuntime.js
    • Owns browser Web Audio constructor detection so playback submodules can no-op safely outside browser audio environments.
  • src/audio/proceduralSounds.js
    • Owns generated short SFX such as countdown beeps and doorbell tones; src/audio/playback.jsx keeps compatibility re-exports for existing callers.
  • src/audio/backgroundMusic.jsx
    • Owns the React BackgroundMusic component, BGM Web Audio scheduling, intro-to-loop timing, decoded BGM buffer cache, autoplay resume fallback, and reconnect recovery guard. src/audio/playback.jsx keeps compatibility re-exports for existing callers.
  • src/audio/effectPlayback.js
    • Owns effect preloading/decoded cache, effect Web Audio playback, HTMLAudio fallback, no-browser no-op protection, board sound routing, and named UI interaction playback helpers. Fixed asset constants live in src/shared/audioAssets.js, including the dedicated home entry effects ui_resume_open.ogg, ui_warehouse_open.ogg, ui_watch_open.ogg, ui_friends_open.ogg, and ui_leaderboard_open.ogg; src/audio/playback.jsx keeps compatibility re-exports for existing callers.
    • src/app/InteractionFeedback.jsx installs document-level interaction feedback: enabled button-like controls play the confirm SFX, disabled/locked controls play the unavailable SFX, and the unavailable target receives a ui-unavailable-shake animation whose duration matches ui_unavailable.ogg. The unavailable animation restarts by removing the class and re-adding it on the next animation frame, avoiding synchronous layout reads on high-frequency disabled taps. Specialized interactions can set data-ui-sound="none" to avoid double-playing the generic confirm cue. Room board interactions, player-info panels, the room title/info stack, and replay control bars are quiet zones for generic confirm SFX so normal moves, high-frequency info taps, and replay stepping do not fire UI prompt sounds; replay disabled buttons can still fire the unavailable SFX.
  • src/audio/playback.jsx
    • Owns voice playback, preloaded voice buffers, TTS, and compatibility re-exports for the split audio modules.
  • BGM 资源配置集中在 MUSIC_TRACKS，按 home、battle、skill 三类管理。
  • 用户音乐库存与选择使用 User.ownedMusicIds 和 User.musicSelections 持久化；公开用户信息会自动并入 defaultUnlocked 曲目。角色普通技能 BGM 继续保存在 musicSelections.skill[characterId]，派生技则按效果类型独立保存在 musicSelections.derivedSkill[characterId][effectType]。解析器按 characterId + musicEffectType 精确匹配槽位，不能直接拿玩法 effectType 当音乐槽位：普通技能预览写入 musicEffectType: ""，派生技能预览和最新技能历史写入对应派生效果类型。选中曲目必须属于该角色、该音乐效果类型且已拥有；无有效选择时依次回退技能定义的 musicTrackId 和该槽第一首可用曲目。旧用户只有 skill 字段时无需迁移；旧派生技能历史缺少 musicEffectType 时可由 musicTrackId 对应音轨的 effectType 兼容推断。
  • 角色详情弹窗右上角包含独立的角色 BGM 预览播放器。普通技与每个配置了曲目的派生技显示为独立技能页签，当前技能槽位使用自己的曲目列表和选择；切换技能页签或曲目时，如果预览正在播放，新曲会自动接续播放，未播放时不会自动起播。选择通过 /api/me/music-selection 持久化，派生技请求附带 effectType，保存失败时播放器内联提示并回滚到上一次有效曲目，且可就地重试。
  • Character detail BGM preview enters an immediate loading UI state before first-use WebAudio fetch/decode completes, keeps aria-busy on the local player during startup, and releases the global background-music pause request if startup fails or the selected track changes. Its shared structure is a compact cassette player with a CSS play/pause/loading glyph, a single-line title, a slot marker, and a fixed-position track sheet rendered into the nearest modal backdrop (with the themed app shell as fallback), keeping the sheet above nested detail content without losing theme ancestry; Bright School adds the paper-grid skin plus the Rough.js frame, key ring, and title rules after mount. Overflowing titles use measured one-way marquee motion with start/end pauses, while short titles stay still; reduced-motion users receive a non-animated horizontally scrollable title instead. The sheet keeps at most about four rows before internal scrolling, remains open after selection, and closes on trigger, outside press, or Escape with focus restoration.
  • 角色 BGM 预览播放期间会向全局 BackgroundMusic 发出暂停请求；背景 BGM 会停止当前 source、保存 offset，并在预览暂停、切歌或关闭详情弹窗释放请求后按 offset 重新调度，从原进度恢复，避免双 BGM 叠播或大厅 BGM 从头重启。
  • 主界面 BGM 由 MUSIC_TRACKS 中可用的 home 曲目池解析；默认池包含单文件循环 home-default（/assets/music/main_bgm.ogg）和两段式 home-main-bgm-1（/assets/music/main_bgm_1_once.ogg 播放一次后接 /assets/music/main_bgm_1_loop.ogg 循环）。useBackgroundMusicTrack() 在每次进入 view === "home" 时抽取一次随机值，本次停留在 home 期间保持同一曲目，离开并再次进入 home 时重新抽取；旧的 musicSelections.home 字段不参与首页随机解析，避免已有资料把大厅 BGM 固定到单曲。
  • 对弈常规 BGM 使用 shanjifu_intro_once.ogg + shanjifu_loop.ogg。
  • 角色技能 BGM 当前配置：达妮娅使用 canonical denia-skill-default（bgm_*），西格莉卡使用 sigrika_intro_once.ogg + sigrika_loop.ogg，爱弥斯“小爱出击”使用 aemeath0_once.ogg + aemeath0_loop.ogg，爱弥斯“远航星”使用 lhl_intro_once.ogg + lhl_loop.ogg，猪小仙使用 matoya_*，娜波摩使用 busizhe_*，仇远默认使用 qiuyuan_intro_once.ogg + qiuyuan_loop.ogg，仇远第二版可购买技能 BGM qiuyuan-skill-zhouwo 使用 /assets/music/qiuyuan_zhouwo_loop.ogg 单文件循环，商城商品图使用 /assets/items/qiuyuan-zhouwo.webp，启动内置商城 seed 会为已存在且 imageUrl 为空的该音乐商品回填图片且不覆盖非空后台配置，千咲使用 chisa_intro_once.ogg + chisa_loop.ogg，长离使用 changli_intro_once.ogg + changli_loop.ogg，莫宁使用 mornye_intro_once.ogg + mornye_loop.ogg。旧达妮娅 slug 会在启动清理阶段迁移/删除，运行时 BGM resolver 只按 canonical denia 查找达妮娅技能 BGM。
  • BGM 优先级为：角色技能 BGM > 对弈常规 BGM > 主界面 BGM。
  • 角色释放技能后，后续 BGM 保持为该角色专属 BGM，直到对局结束；后触发的角色技能 BGM 会覆盖先触发的角色技能 BGM。普通技能即使拥有 flip-stone、liberty-purge 等非空玩法 effectType，也必须读取角色普通技能槽位；只有明确带有非空 musicEffectType 的派生技能才读取派生槽位。
  • 匹配成功倒计时、对局结果弹窗、对局房间的结束阶段会停止 BGM；如果用户从已结束棋谱回放退出到大厅，前端会清空回放房间快照，BGM resolver 以当前 view = home 为准恢复主界面 BGM。在线玩家从已结束房间主动退出回大厅时，前端也会把该房间号记为已处理结果，防止房间 5 分钟回收前的残留终局快照继续触发结果弹窗状态并压住大厅 BGM。
  • 持久登录自动进入大厅时可能没有用户手势，浏览器会继续挂起 AudioContext；BackgroundMusic 会在 resume 后检查上下文状态，如果仍为 suspended，就注册 pointerdown / keydown / touchstart 一次性重试，保证首次点击、按键或触摸后恢复大厅 BGM。
  • BackgroundMusic 使用 Web Audio 播放，预解码 intro/loop buffer，并在同一 AudioContext 时间线上调度 intro 到 loop，避免 HTMLAudio 切文件造成明显卡顿。
  • 组件级 BackgroundMusic 卸载时必须停止 WebAudio source 与 HTMLAudio fallback、清空当前曲目并废弃未完成调度，避免教学对弈、预览层等局部界面退出后残留 BGM。
  • 音量变化只调节 gain，不改变播放 identity；因此主音量或 BGM 音量调到 0 后再恢复，不会导致 BGM 从头播放。
  • 新用户默认音频设置为 master: 100、bgm: 60、sfx: 100、voice: 100，保存于 localStorage 的 sigrika-audio-settings；已有持久化百分比继续覆盖这些默认值。运行时音量计算会把非法或非有限百分比值回退到默认值，避免损坏的本地设置把 NaN 传入 Audio/Web Audio。
  • audioRuntime.js 通过统一 helper 暴露浏览器 AudioContext 构造器；倒计时 beep、门铃音和 TTS 在缺少 window、AudioContext 或 speechSynthesis 时静默 no-op，避免测试、预渲染或非浏览器执行路径因音频副作用抛错。
  • 匹配成功后先播放原有 3 秒匹配成功倒计时弹窗，倒计时结束后才进入对局资源预加载页并停止 BGM；该页复用登录加载页样式，但不显示转圈动画，只显示本机玩家出战角色跳动立绘、该角色的加载台词，以及 资源加载中 0/2、1/2 或 2/2，客户端完成本地资源预载后发出 room:preload-ready。进入对局页需要同时满足匹配成功倒计时完成和双方都 ready：倒计时先结束时进入并继续停在资源加载页，资源先 ready 时继续显示倒计时并在倒计时结束后直接进入对局页；服务端 90 秒未等齐会中止匹配并由前端 toast 一方加载超时，匹配中止。
  • 对局结果弹窗出现时停止 BGM；胜方播放 result-victory.mp3，负方播放 result-defeat.mp3，和棋不播放结果音效。房间玩家还会按当前用户本局结果触发出战角色的 result-victory、result-defeat 或 result-draw 系统语音；琳奈的 lynaeContraryVoice 生效时，结算使用服务端清除前写入的 completedItemEffects 快照将胜利与失败语音互换，界面结果与胜负音效仍保持真实结果，和棋语音不变。无效局不弹结果弹窗，因此不触发结果音效或结果角色语音。棋谱回放模式不会弹结果弹窗，也不会触发结果音效或结果角色语音。
  • 普通落子播放 godown_clear.ogg。
  • 提子动作播放 go_capture_clear.ogg。
  • 隐藏手暴露播放 hidden_hand_reveal.ogg；回放下一步遇到带有隐藏手暴露标记的历史动作时也会播放该音效。
  • boardAudio.js 根据历史记录中的当前棋盘动作判断落子音或提子音；回放界面点击“下一手”时也会按该步历史播放对应音效，弃手不会重复播放上一手棋盘音效。
  • 落子、提子、隐藏手暴露、匹配成功和结果音效均走 sfx 音量通道。

  • 星炬棋盘技能特效的程序化音效由 src/room/boardSkillEffectSoundScheduler.js 按 catalog cue 点调度，并由 src/audio/skillEffectSounds.js 负责真正发声；这些音效只读 audioSettings.sfx，不参与服务端结算，也不会在 reduced-motion 棋盘演出中播放爆炸/飞入类音效。src/shared/skillPresentation.js 会先判断该技能演出和音效层是否启用，关闭特效时不进入 SFX 调度；后台 skillEffectsEnabled 关闭后，服务端 pending skill preview 也只等待技能横幅时长，随后直接广播技能结算结果。src/room/pixiPrewarm.js 在技能可用棋盘空闲时预热 pixi.js，真实演出时复用同一个加载 promise，避免首个技能把模块加载成本集中到动画开始时；关闭特效或无技能棋盘不会为技能演出预热 Pixi。
  • row-slash 使用 skillEffectSoundCues cue 元数据调度程序化技能音效；技能横幅结束后通过 BoardSkillEffects 预热并播放不依赖图片资源的 Pixi Graphics 水墨刀光，结算后的持久棋盘刀痕仍走 DOM/CSS overlay，没有独立刀光图片或音频文件依赖。protocol-takeover 使用程序化淡紫与冰蓝数据流 Pixi 光束演出，并通过 WebAudio 程序化播放更明显的两段式数据束启动与目标锁定 SFX，结算后的协议禁入标记继续走 DOM/CSS overlay。double-move 使用程序化火焰 SFX，并把凤凰与火焰透明 SVG 加入 RUNTIME_IMAGE_ASSETS.effects 延迟预加载，技能横幅后的全盘演出通过 Pixi 复用纹理，实际连落点位继续通过 DOM/CSS 显示持续火焰。
  • 娜波摩被动灰白化层由 BoardAmbientEffects 根据 game.passives.*.colorIllusion.active 派生，使用 pointer-transparent 的 CSS 中心向外扩散波纹表现发动过程，同时 .board-wrap.color-illusion-board-surface::before 会把棋盘背景从中心扩散切换到 /assets/boards/nabomo-color-illusion-board.webp；该专用 WebP 由 C:/codex/image/go-board-background-reference-color-vertical-2048.jpg 转换而来，并作为效果资源延迟预加载。该被动不再使用持续 Pixi 烟雾或每帧 ticker 绘制，也不允许回退为整块矩形半透明贴层，因此不会影响落子、触控确认、数子标记或观战/回放棋盘读取。
  • 读秒 10 到 1 秒倒计时通过 playSystemVoice(countdown-N) 播放角色语音或 TTS；useRoomAudioEffects 不再为最后 10 秒申请 BGM duck，单个片段也不会改变 BGM 音量。解码倒计时语音继续使用无混响的 countdown playback profile；超时只作为结算状态，不播放系统音频、角色语音或“还剩0次读秒”。
  • 角色技能语音配置集中在 CHARACTER_SKILL_VOICES，派生技能等效果特定语音通过 SYSTEM_VOICE_SKILL_EVENTS 的模式化事件覆盖普通 skill-cast。
  • CHARACTER_SKILL_VOICES 支持单个路径或候选路径数组；resolveSystemVoice 每次解析数组候选时随机选择一个，loginPreloadAssets() 与 battlePreloadAssets() 会展开候选数组，保证所有可能播放的语音都纳入预加载。
  • 当前已配置：西格莉卡技能发动使用 sigrika_skill_cast.ogg，爱弥斯普通技能使用 aemeath_skill_cast.ogg，爱弥斯“远航星”通过 skill-cast:voyage-star 使用 aemeath_skill_cast_voyage.ogg，娜波摩使用 nabomo_skill_cast.ogg，猪小仙使用 baconbits_skill_cast.ogg，琳奈使用 lynae_skill_cast.ogg，长离使用 changli_skill_cast.ogg，千咲使用 chisa_skill_cast.ogg，莫宁使用 mornye_skill_cast.ogg。
  • 仇远技能发动语音使用候选 qiuyuan_skill_cast.ogg 与 qiuyuan_skill_cast_1.ogg，每次释放技能随机播放其中一个。
  • characterVoiceMapForSkill 可将现有技能语音桥接为角色 systemVoices 的 skill-cast 事件映射。
  • 角色出战按钮会触发 sortie 角色语音事件；当前达妮娅、西格莉卡、猪小仙、琳奈、长离、千咲和莫宁已分别接入 denia_sortie.ogg、sigrika_sortie.ogg、baconbits_sortie.ogg、lynae_sortie.ogg、changli_sortie.ogg、chisa_sortie.ogg 与 mornye_sortie.ogg。
  • SkillBanner 出现时同步触发技能语音，同一个 banner id 只播放一次。
  • 技能语音走 voice 音量通道。
  • playVoiceSound 使用 Web Audio 播放链：source -> RMS normalization gain -> dry/wet reverb mix -> voice gain -> destination。
  • 同一客户端只允许一个角色语音处于活动播放状态；新的角色语音或 TTS 开始前会立即停止上一段角色语音，避免同一用户连续触发角色语音时叠音。
  • 莫宁（mornye）已接入 game-start、sortie、skill-cast、byo-yomi-start、byo-yomi-period-2、byo-yomi-period-1、countdown-10 到 countdown-1、result-victory、result-defeat、result-draw 与 house-detail 语音；house-detail 使用 /assets/voice/mornye_detail.ogg，打开角色详情或点击详情内“描述”文本区域都会播放该语音，关闭角色详情弹窗或关闭整个部员手册会调用 stopVoicePlayback() 停止当前角色语音。
  • 角色音频和 TTS 播放不再通知 BGM ducking 状态，也不改变背景音乐 gain；BGM 保持用户设置音量。backgroundDucking.js 只处理招募演出等非语音场景显式申请的 scoped request。
  • voiceEffects.js 当前预设为轻量空灵混响：boost: 1.35、wet: 0.28、dry: 0.9、reverbSeconds: 1.6、reverbDecay: 2.2、preDelaySeconds: 0.035，并以 targetRms: 0.12、minNormalizationGain: 0.4、maxNormalizationGain: 2.4 做运行时语音响度一致化。普通语音使用完整 dry/wet 链，连续倒计时解码语音只走归一化后的干声支路，避免一秒一段的混响尾音堆叠；普通 Audio fallback 本身无 Web Audio 混响。
  • 如果 Web Audio 或资源加载失败，技能语音会回退到普通 Audio 播放，仍应用 1.35 倍 voice 增益上限。
  • 系统语音事件集中在 src/shared/systemVoices.js，通过 resolveSystemVoice 解析。
  • 琳奈的 lynaeContraryVoice 由 resolver 在选取音频前做固定事件重映射：countdown-N 映射为 countdown-(11-N)，byo-yomi-period-2 ↔ byo-yomi-period-1、game-start ↔ byo-yomi-start、sortie ↔ skill-cast、result-victory ↔ result-defeat；result-draw、house-detail 与超时静音保持原样。房间语音角色、技能横幅、棋舍出战和结果弹窗都必须显式携带对应的 itemEffects，不能修改静态角色目录或真实事件数据。
  • 当前默认走 TTS 文本；如果角色配置了 systemVoices[event]，则优先播放对应音频。
  • 已预留事件：game-start、game-start:gomoku、skill-cast、sortie、byo-yomi-start、byo-yomi-periods、byo-yomi-period-2、byo-yomi-period-1、byo-yomi-countdown、countdown-N、timeout、result-victory、result-defeat、result-draw、house-detail。
  • 对局正式开始时，服务端写入 kind 为 game-start 的系统消息，前端据此播放“对局开始”语音。
  • game-start:gomoku 是 game-start 的模式化角色语音变体；当前长离在五子棋模式开局时优先播放 changli_wuzi_match_start.ogg，其它模式继续播放 changli_match_start.ogg；长离倒计时已覆盖 10 到 1 秒。
  • 进入读秒和剩余读秒次数已经接入事件化 resolver；剩余 2/1 次读秒使用显式 byo-yomi-period-2、byo-yomi-period-1 事件，10 到 1 秒倒计时使用 countdown-10 到 countdown-1 角色语音事件。timeout 不再解析为角色音频或 TTS 文本。
  • BGM 和效果音位于 public/assets/music/。
  • 技能语音位于 public/assets/voice/。
  • 转码用临时工具目录 .tmp/ 已加入 .gitignore，不应提交。
  • Runtime audio playback now lives across focused src/audio/ modules: audio settings, BGM ducking, browser audio runtime detection, procedural generated sounds, background music scheduling, and effect playback are split out, while playback.jsx keeps voice/TTS helpers plus compatibility re-exports. Future cleanup can continue splitting voice and audio-cache concerns if the feature set grows.
  • Voice playback uses a shared module-level Web Audio context when available; regular voice playback, cached playback, and preload decoding all reuse that context. Future tuning may still need more explicit lifecycle cleanup and browser autoplay handling.
  • Audio settings, background music ducking, runtime voice loudness helpers, and no-browser audio API guards are covered by deterministic helper tests, but not by a browser-level audio graph test.
  • Runtime voice loudness normalization uses decoded AudioBuffer RMS statistics and clamps correction gain between 0.4 and 2.4, so authored assets can remain unchanged while character voices play at a closer perceived level.
  • Voice reverb now uses a deterministic generated impulse. If authored reverb tails become important, replace it with a static impulse asset.
  • BGM assets are committed directly under public/assets/music/ and increase repository size. If the soundtrack grows, consider Git LFS, an asset CDN, or a manifest-driven asset pipeline.
  • Music ownership, purchase availability, and player selection are represented in configuration shape but not yet backed by persisted music inventory/settings UI.
  • Character skill voice configuration is static in CHARACTER_SKILL_VOICES; future admin-configurable voices would need schema/API/upload support and cache invalidation rules.
  • No browser-level automated test currently verifies actual audio playback, Web Audio graph routing, or autoplay fallback behavior; existing coverage focuses on deterministic helper logic.
  • Countdown character voice assets are opportunistically preloaded and decoded into a module-level Web Audio buffer cache while the active player is in byo-yomi. Cached playback reuses the same dry/wet reverb voice chain as regular voice playback, and failures fall back to the existing voice path without blocking timers.

Music Display Name Management

  • Current effective preload contract: login preload blocks home entry only on the selected character portrait, home-shell images, interaction audio, and currently reachable home BGM. Other owned character portraits, shop/recruitment/inventory/equipment/decorations, battle/skill tracks, result sounds, and character voices remain in the same manifest but load in the bounded background queue after home entry. Unpurchased music products still do not preload full audio. Battle preload is scoped to the current room and current user's music selections: it loads both player portraits, only the selected battle track, one resolved track per relevant base/derived skill slot, required voice candidates, and mode-specific effect images. No-skill modes such as standard and gomoku skip skill BGM, skill voices, and skill effect images. A refreshed or reconnected player that resumes a room still in GAME_PHASES.preloading must return to the battle preload route and emit room:preload-ready after local assets finish, instead of entering active room state from that snapshot; this report is retried until the server acknowledges it.
  • Client-side playable-ready prewarm sits before the current effective preload contract. It can warm module/Pixi caches while the user is on the home or match-selection surfaces, but the app still shows match-preloading and waits for the same room-scoped battle asset manifest plus server ready acknowledgements before exposing the interactive room.
  • Current effective cache contract: production gives hashed Vite build outputs Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable; named /assets/** runtime resources and the SPA shell use no-cache. Nginx and Express fallback templates share these values, so stable-name WebP/SVG/BGM/voice replacements revalidate through ETag/Last-Modified instead of serving a stale visual for another release window. Hash or version filenames remain recommended for truly immutable high-traffic resources.
  • Local stability verification uses LOCAL_PROD_STATIC=1 to exercise this same cache contract against the production build without setting NODE_ENV=production. This keeps local socket/auth/test-action behavior usable while still catching stale-cache regressions before a cloud deploy. npm run verify:stability now also covers desktop/mobile room recovery, real Pixi .board-effects-canvas playback for Sigrika and Aemeath hidden-hand / voyage-star, finished-room exit plus reload without reopening the result modal, and opponent reconnect without showing the local player a rate-limit toast.
  • Startup preload may show the generic login preloading route only while no room or pending match has already been restored by Socket.IO. If roomRef.current or matchSuccessRef.current exists after /api/me, startup asset loading continues without hiding the recovered battle route.
  • MUSIC_TRACKS remains the authority for playable BGM ids, types, character bindings, default unlock flags, purchasability, playback modes, and audio source paths. Home BGM tracks share the same static catalog: adding a new default-unlocked or owned home track automatically makes it eligible for home-entry randomization and login preload.
  • MusicTrackSetting stores only per-track displayName overrides keyed by static track id. Blank or missing settings fall back to the static track.name.
  • Server startup runs ensureMusicTrackSettingsSchema() so older SQLite development databases can read and write music display-name settings without a manual migration.
  • Authenticated players read the merged catalog from GET /api/music-tracks; admin users read and update the same catalog through GET /api/admin/music-tracks and PATCH /api/admin/music-tracks/:id.
  • The admin music tab edits display names only. It cannot create tracks, upload or replace audio files, disable tracks, or change track type/character binding.
  • Frontend music consumers should pass the merged track map into existing music-library resolver functions instead of duplicating selection logic. Character BGM preview/select controls, background music resolution, shop music card names, and gacha music prize options/display names should all use the merged catalog.

Login Asset Preloading

  • Frontend deployment helpers live in src/shared/preloadAssets.js.
  • Socket.IO now connects to window.location.origin, so the deployed site can run behind https://sigrika.fun without a hard-coded localhost socket endpoint.
  • Vite development proxy forwards /socket.io websocket traffic to the local backend, keeping the same-origin socket path usable in development and production.
  • After a valid token is confirmed, the app enters a preloading view before the home screen.
  • Fresh login enters preloading before the home screen, preventing the home screen from flashing before assets begin loading. Stored-token startup is intentionally disabled so refresh and browser restart return to the login screen.
  • The preload step fetches non-replay runtime assets after login and blocks home entry on the current user's accessible asset manifest. That manifest includes current-user-visible character portraits, home entry/background imagery, shop product display images, owned inventory/recruitment item imagery, equipped achievement asset images, owned/default-unlocked BGM sources, relevant owned/default character skill BGM, character skill voices, system voices, and common board/UI effect sounds. Unpurchased music products only contribute shop display imagery, not full BGM audio, because the player cannot preview unowned music. Replay lists and replay details remain lazy data requests so opening the app does not prefetch historical game records.

Aemeath Voyage Star Resources

  • voyage-star is a full-board Pixi skill effect backed by /assets/effects/voyage-star-crater.webp, a transparent WebP converted from the supplied heart/star artwork. src/room/boardSkillEffectRegistry.js draws the white/ice-blue knight greatsword, target omen, impact glow, visual-only quake layer, yellow-white soft-edged explosion cover, and removal particles from pending preview geometry while leaving the crater artwork to the resolved board overlay. The greatsword drops from above with a short trail and stays visible through impact; the impact window adds concentric earthquake ellipses, 2-3 decaying shake pulses on the Pixi layer only, and deterministic dust sparks without moving the DOM board or hit areas. The explosion cover layer is added after the other Pixi layers so it can fully mask the impact frame; it expands from the skill center with a solid warm-white core whose radius is at least 2.2 board cells, covering the roughly four-cell crater footprint, then blends outward through low-alpha white and warm-yellow discs whose feathered outer edge fades to zero alpha. The separate impact glow fades before full-cover, then the renderer adds a fully opaque white full-board cover with no circular rim, holds that cover for resolution, and dissolves it with eased opacity plus outward yellow-white rings, radial streaks, and spark fragments instead of snapping directly back to the board; the sword alpha is forced to zero once that cover reaches every board corner. The Pixi cast starts from procedural Graphics and never draws a temporary crater sprite or fallback shape; the crater WebP is loaded through the renderer asset list only as monitored prewarm for the resolved DOM crater, so slow image decode cannot delay the real Pixi canvas or the full-board cast startup. skillPreviewResolutionDelay({ effectType: "voyage-star" }) resolves at 2936ms, which is the 2s banner plus 52% of the 1800ms board-effect window, placing the authoritative crater and stone removals under the full-cover explosion light. BoardSkillEffects keeps an already-started Pixi cast alive after that early resolved snapshot clears pendingSkill, so the opaque cover hides the resolved board until its dissolve phase instead of letting the crater show through the whiteout. The persistent DOM crater is a board-level .voyage-star-crater-mark overlay positioned by board-coordinate CSS variables from the source id, sized to 3.5 board cells, and animated with tight pink-purple/cyan drop-shadow glow that follows the WebP alpha shape instead of a round haze; the erased points remain stored on the points as voyage-star-crater-point or voyage-star-erased-point. This one-owner split keeps the crater from appearing inside the cover, avoids a sudden post-effect crater pop, and avoids a second flash when the resolved room state arrives.
  • aemeath-skill-default is the selectable base Aemeath “小爱出击” BGM and uses /assets/music/aemeath0_once.ogg followed by /assets/music/aemeath0_loop.ogg. aemeath-voyage-star-default belongs to the independent voyage-star derived slot and uses /assets/music/lhl_intro_once.ogg followed by /assets/music/lhl_loop.ogg; Voyage Star pending previews and resolved skill history carry musicEffectType: "voyage-star", so findSkillTrack() reads musicSelections.derivedSkill.aemeath["voyage-star"] before falling back to that configured track. Ordinary Aemeath previews carry an empty music effect type and continue to read musicSelections.skill.aemeath. battlePreloadAssets() preloads derived skill tracks by effectType, and old Voyage Star history without musicEffectType remains compatible because its musicTrackId resolves to track metadata with effectType: "voyage-star". Future purchasable Aemeath tracks use the character-level slot only when they replace “小爱出击”; tracks replacing Voyage Star or another derived skill use the existing effect-scoped nested selection.
  • The Voyage Star track intentionally keeps Aemeath's previous lhl_* intro/loop files, while the base Aemeath track now uses the supplied aemeath0_* assets.
  • Preload failures are non-blocking per resource: failed or hanging asset loaders are skipped after a bounded per-task timeout so users are not trapped on the loading screen if a single asset stalls during reconnect, server restart, or cache recovery. Skipped login and battle assets are retried in the background after the target screen is entered with a lower concurrency cap.
  • The preload screen uses a character portrait hop plus progress bar, with a short minimum display duration to avoid a visual flash on cached loads. AssetPreloadScreen reads siteSettings.characterLoadingLines as characterId=line rows for the main loading line, uses a random catalog character for post-login preload, and uses the current player character for battle preload; the spinner remains only as an image-missing fallback. It also reads siteSettings.preloadTips, parses one non-empty tip per line, shows one random tip below the progress bar, and on the post-login preload view rotates the random character portrait, character loading line, and tip together every 10 seconds while the view remains open; fixed-character battle preload keeps the player character stable and only rotates tips. showTips={false} is reserved for flows that must reuse the same loading template but suppress random hints, such as tutorial-battle entry and exit transitions with fixed copy. The loading panel itself stays borderless and transparent in both the base layer and the final Bright School safety layer, uses full-viewport centering, and lets the character line plus tip text wrap inside the shared preload width so mobile and desktop keep the same no-frame loading treatment without adding a solid middle panel background.

---

UI 主题、移动端与交互体验

本文记录 Bright School 主题、CSS 分层、移动端适配、弹窗布局、反馈消息和 UI 交互体验。新增主题、移动布局、弹窗视觉合同或用户反馈界面时优先更新本分篇。

当前结论

  • 玩家侧目前只有 Bright School 一个默认主题，但主题注册、作用域和 CSS 入口仍保留扩展口。
  • src/styles/base.css、room.css、modals.css、commerce-settings.css、responsive.css、mobile-room.css、hud-components.css、mobile-adaptive.css 和后台/剧情教学路由 CSS 都保持 import-only 入口加领域子目录的结构；低频路由 CSS 由 lazy owner 组件导入，最终 mobile-adaptive.css 仍在主题之后作为手机、窄桌面和后台全屏安全层。
  • Bright School 根入口当前顺序是 base.css、gallery-polish.css、surface-contracts.css、component-repairs.css、qa-guard.css。surface-contracts.css 是显式表面合同层，委托到 surface-contracts/ 下的 root/surface/controls/forms/badges、known HUD reset、home/profile/commerce/room cleanup、final explicit surfaces、announcement controls 和 typography 分篇；它替代旧广域兜底清理结构，不再允许 broad substring selector、空兼容文件或主题级 * 阴影清理。
  • Bright School 的高频视觉工作仍进入 owning domain：大厅和主面板在 home/，商业在 commerce/，弹窗/设置/结果/房间浮层在 modals/，技能/棋盘动效在 effects/，对局可读性在 room/，移动端在 mobile/，意图明确的纸张/手账细节恢复在 component-repairs/，质量和移动兜底由 qa-guard.css 后置导入。
  • 低频路由 CSS 不再要求进入全局根样式：src/styles/admin.css 是后台管理 domain entry，但由 lazy src/admin/AdminConsole.jsx 导入；src/styles/room/tutorial-battle-screen.css 是剧情教学对弈专属 entry，由 lazy src/tutorial/TutorialBattleScreen.jsx 导入，包含桌面和移动教学战斗空角色位、目标/行动、独立 target-ring.css 目标光圈和 loading motion 规则。src/styles/mobile-adaptive/admin-fullscreen.css 仍保留在全局最终安全层中，因为它需要在主题和 HUD 之后清理 .admin-screen 的全屏背景继承。
  • 后台管理是浅色生产工具界面，即使复用 .primary-action、.secondary-action、.danger-action、.close-button、.modal-backdrop、.confirm-modal、普通 input/textarea/select 或带 setting 片段的容器，也必须由 src/styles/admin/polish.css 与公告管理样式在 .admin-screen 内显式复位 clip-path、暗色/霓虹背景、绿色终端边线、text-shadow/filter 和终端 pseudo-element；该合同同时覆盖桌面端和移动端，避免 Startorch/HUD 的黑底、绿纹、斜角控件和黑色输入框污染后台。
  • 移动端布局由共享移动层、Bright School 移动层和最终 mobile-adaptive.css 安全层共同约束。设置弹窗在最终手机层使用标题、tab、内部面板三行网格，外壳不滚动，当前 settings-panel 自己滚动并 containment；关闭按钮、首页图标按钮和手机菜单按钮保持显式 accessible label 与 44px 触控目标。
  • 视觉合同应靠 CSS contract tests 锁定，避免后续主题覆盖把语义状态、关闭按钮、移动列表等打散。
  • 商店窗口在同一 ModalDialog 内挂载扎希拉商铺与残星会服装店，两页用 240ms transform 平移切换并在 reduced-motion 下取消过渡；每页保留独立目录、批次、3 秒刷新冷却和错误重试状态。Bright School 竖屏首页的通用 max-width 保护只作用于 .home-screen 后代，不能压缩商店的 200% 双页滑轨。扎希拉 data-store="zahira" 页头使用简单的蓝灰—灰紫低细节配色条，不裁切或模拟主体桌面场景；手机最终层把通用模态框关闭按钮恢复为静态网格项，使关闭和刷新两个 44px 控件在三列页头内垂直居中。服装店使用版本化深红粗蜡笔舞台图，页头标题固定为不换行且完整显示的“残星会cosplay部”；data-store="costume" 的页头复用桌面舞台图顶部帷幕，并以深红、黑梅低对比色块延续粗蜡笔舞台，暖象牙标题与现有浅色纸面按钮在桌面和竖屏共用同一语义 owner。桌面图按左侧约 34% 娜波摩接待、右侧约 65% 最多五件服装的低细节舞台构图，竖屏独立图按上方约 57% 商品舞台、下方约 43% 看板娘/气泡/钱包接待区构图。两张图都使用与扎希拉背景一致的蜡笔颗粒、纸张纹理、歪斜轮廓和简化色块，并以居中 cover 覆盖 .costume-store-panel；最终移动层通过 --costume-shop-mobile-background-image 直接选择竖图，不运行时裁切横图，也不叠加旧幕布伪元素。两端的服装商品舞台都复用扎希拉商店的实际容器测量与 layoutShopCards 数量感知排布，按当前 1–5 件商品动态选择单排或平衡双排、逐行居中且不保留空槽；批次内每件服装复用同一套固定轻微旋转和 4–6px 纵向浮动参数，悬停/聚焦/按下时暂停浮动，减少动态效果时停止连续动画。五张商品图使用裁到 alpha 内容边界的矩形 WebP，.costume-shop-art 随实际立绘尺寸收缩，售价绝对定位到该锚点右下角并与可见立绘少量重叠；立绘与售价共同位于旋转/浮动包装内，始终作为一张商品卡同步变换。桌面悬停保留原位移动效但卡片背景保持透明，只在立绘后方显示局部暖金舞台光。两家商店共用 ShopWallet 的钱包 WebP、金币胶囊和数字/单位排版，只覆盖各自的布局锚点；未拥有对应角色的服装卡只灰化立绘，仍可打开详情，购买按钮不可用。
  • 部员手册角色详情的衣服图标始终可点，桌面位于立绘区右上角、移动端移到左上角以避开关闭按钮。服装选择通过 portal 挂到主题应用根并使用独立顶层遮罩，不受部员手册父窗口的布局与裁剪影响；窗口只显示“{角色名}的衣柜”标题，不显示副标题、“只有默认服装”等解释性文案；列表固定“默认服装”第一、当前非默认服装第二、其他已拥有服装随后、未拥有服装最后。卡片主体打开详情且必须保持透明、无边框和无额外阴影，让外层卡片承担与部员手册角色卡片一致的纸卡轮廓；独立衣服按钮即时装扮。当前服装仅以外层浅绿色卡片背景表达，并通过 aria-current 保留语义，不显示“装扮中”文案。非默认服装的衣柜缩略图与全局实际装扮立绘读取同一套逐服装缩放/偏移校准；校准使用独立 CSS transform 属性与既有交互动效合成，不改变卡片布局盒，也不作用于商店商品图和详情大图。未拥有服装与未拥有角色的装扮按钮使用原生 disabled，不增加额外提示；默认服装不灰化。移动端规则由 modals/character-opening/costume-wardrobe-mobile.css 单独拥有，服装网格为两列、内部滚动，卡内立绘与装扮按钮纵向排列并水平居中；服装详情使用应用根下的第二个固定定位顶层遮罩，与衣柜窗口互为同级，不能作为衣柜内部的相对定位子层。
  • Board move preview and mobile touch-confirmation markers must stay centered by the .point absolute coordinate contract: each point receives --board-point-center-x/y, sits on the same intersection as SVG grid lines, and centers its preview pseudo-element internally. The shared .board .point rule repeats transform: translate(-50%, -50%) !important so later generic button hover/active transforms cannot shift board points into grid cells. ChangLi's extra-turn placements reuse ordinary move placement hints, so they must stay on the same intersections as stones and SVG grid lines. Mobile touch confirmation must render only the inner .touch-confirm-marker; .point.touch-confirming::before stays hidden, and confirmation feedback may brighten the point but must not drop the centering transform. If a mobile press state scales a point, it must compose with translate(-50%, -50%), and reduced-motion states must keep the same translate so stones do not animate from grid cells back to intersections after placement.
  • Tailwind CSS is available only as a low-intrusion utility layer through src/styles/tailwind.css, imported from src/styles.css after hud-components.css and immediately before src/styles/themes.css. It uses the Tailwind v4 Vite plugin, imports tailwindcss/theme.css, src/styles/tailwind/tokens.css, and tailwindcss/utilities.css with prefix(tw) on Tailwind imports and source("../") on the utilities import, and deliberately omits preflight so Bright School, board, modal, and mobile safety layers keep their existing element and component contracts. Tailwind is a staged long-term target with its full execution plan persisted at .trellis/tasks/07-01-tailwind-migration-roadmap/tailwind-full-migration-plan.md: phase 1 records contracts and the @theme inline semantic token scaffold without migrating JSX, phase 2 pilots tw: utilities in new or isolated admin/tooling surfaces, phase 3 creates UI primitives, phase 4 migrates shared modal/list/card/form internals, phase 5 migrates home/lobby/commerce non-gameplay flows, phase 6 tokenises Bright School, and phase 7 reduces final mobile safety layers only after component ownership and visual checks exist. The current Phase 2 pilot is limited to admin table shells: AdminAudit first proved the tw:max-w-full/tw:overflow-x-auto utility path, and Phase 3 now routes all admin .admin-table-wrap shells through AdminTableScroll backed by src/ui/primitives/ScrollArea.jsx while existing CSS keeps visual shell rules. Phase 3 also includes src/ui/primitives/Badge.jsx for low-risk inline status badge layout utilities, src/ui/primitives/EmptyState.jsx for admin table empty-cell alignment/spacing through AdminTableEmpty, and src/ui/primitives/Button.jsx for admin action alignment through AdminActionButton, with visual styling still owned by existing CSS. Phase 4 has started only with src/modals/modalComponents.jsx ModalActionButton, used by ConfirmModal, MessageBoardModal, simple AnnouncementModal retry/load-more actions, the PersonalizationModal save action, the MailboxModal attachment claim action, the FriendsOverlays duel-mode cancel action, and the UserProfileCard profile report submit action to route modal action alignment through Button while preserving existing .danger-action, .secondary-action, and .primary-action visual contracts. Current room gameplay, board geometry, skill presentation, Pixi canvas hosts, player-facing button restyling, mailbox list rows/delete controls, friends match-mode option buttons, profile confirm panels, profile social action buttons, Bright School final mobile gameplay safety, and mobile gameplay controls stay CSS-entry owned.
  • Current Phase 5 has started only with src/home/homeComponents.jsx HomeActionButton, used by the home match-mode picker cancel action to route alignment through Button while preserving the existing .secondary-action visual contract. The match-mode option buttons, modal spacing contracts, home artboard, utility entries, player plaque art, decorative imagery, and final mobile safety rules remain CSS-owned until a full home/lobby/commerce surface has focused desktop/mobile tests and visual checks.
  • Current Phase 6 has started only as a no-visual-change token contract: src/styles/tailwind/tokens.css maps Bright School paper, clean surface, ink, border, accent, and paper-shadow variables into Tailwind semantic tokens, while Bright School value ownership remains in surface-contracts/final-root-surfaces.css and quality-base/refinement-foundation.css. Current Phase 7 has started only as cssLayerInventory registration for the mobile-adaptive.css final guard reduction candidate; the final mobile layer remains after themes.css until replacement component ownership is proven on phone portrait, small landscape, narrow desktop, and regular desktop.
  • Accent typography is semantic and opt-in. src/styles/base/foundation.css owns "Sigrika Accent Latin" plus --font-display-accent, --font-numeric-accent, .text-display-accent, .text-clock-value, and .text-rating-value; the font is not a global UI replacement. Chess clock digits use .text-clock-value, player-visible rating values use .text-rating-value, and only player-side atmosphere labels such as the login SigrikaGo subtitle use .text-display-accent. Home student-ID plaque rank digits are a narrow owner-CSS exception: .plaque-mode-rank uses --font-numeric-accent directly for the displayed number only, without applying .text-rating-value to non-rating data. These accent classes use text-transform: uppercase so lowercase Latin source text visually renders as uppercase art lettering without mutating the underlying string. Usernames/UserIdentity, admin, body copy, chat/announcements, form inputs, coins, prices, stock, probabilities, game-count/record numbers, room codes, move counts, dates, timestamps, and leaderboard rank positions keep the normal UI font. Because Bright School has late broad readability guards with !important, src/styles/mobile-adaptive/semantic-accent-typography.css is the final post-theme guard that reapplies the semantic font tokens and uppercase transform, restores clock state colors and timer track variables, clears accidental background/border/shadow from .text-clock-value, keeps main-time clock digits at dark #1c171a instead of gray while preserving byo-yomi warning colors, deliberately avoids changing .digital-timer card background or border styling, and applies a mobile-only compact clock sizing override so player-strip timer tracks stay inside their container after the art font loads.
  • Window-title typography is a separate semantic contract. src/styles/base/foundation.css registers public/assets/fonts/LXGWMarkerGothic-Regular.ttf as "Sigrika Window Title" and exposes --font-window-title; .text-window-title applies it to the home main title, while dialog/backdrop-scoped h2 selectors cover player-facing window titles without changing body copy, controls, subtitles, or numeric text. The final semantic typography guard repeats the title family with !important so Bright School's broad Microsoft YaHei reset cannot overwrite it on desktop or mobile.
  • Startorch home terminal styles are now an import-only lobby skin entry. src/styles/home-terminal.css owns import order and delegates concrete rules to src/styles/home-terminal/shell-background.css, top-strip.css, layout-player.css, entries.css, utility-footer-motion.css, and mobile.css; src/styles/styleContract.test.js guards that directory and import map.
  • Shared mobile home layout styles are now import-only as well. src/styles/mobile-home.css delegates the base 900px mobile/tablet rules, the 620px narrow-phone fallback, and the 900px landscape fallback to src/styles/mobile-home/, with src/styles/styleContract.test.js guarding the import order.
  • Bright School portrait mobile home shell is now a nested import-only entry: src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/home-shell.css owns import order, while src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/home-shell/ contains shell-base, top-strip-menu, main-stage, player-plaque, and entries-utility-footer so future mobile home, menu, plaque, and footer rules stay in focused files.
  • Bright School phone portrait home must remain usable in portrait. The portrait home shell displays the main stage as a single-column flow (player, match, manual, utility) and keeps the GPT Image 2 utility dock as a 2x3 image-only button grid with native button semantics and aria-labels; it must not hide the stage behind an orientation guard. The phone-only match and member-handbook image tracks stay compressed so the utility dock remains inside the mobile main-panel frame, but the alpha-trimmed match entry owns the larger track (clamp(176px, 52vw, 224px)) and the handbook entry owns the smaller secondary track (clamp(148px, 42vw, 188px)) so matching cannot appear smaller than 部员手册; each utility button keeps a 52px-plus touch target.
  • Bright School 首页主面板背景由设备图源驱动：桌面主面板使用 /assets/home/home-main-panel-desktop.webp，移动/竖屏使用 /assets/home/home-main-panel-mobile.webp。图源挂在 .home-main-panel.home-terminal-main 上并以 background-size: 100% 100% 跟随面板宽高伸缩；mobile-adaptive/home-narrow-desktop/ 的 1024-1180px 与 701-1023px 兜底仍保留横版图的木框/纸面安全内边距，手机 portrait 的 mobile/home-shell/main-stage.css 负责切换竖版背景和安全内边距，内部 .home-stage 继续透明。
  • Bright School desktop student ID card styling is now a nested import-only entry: src/styles/themes/bright-school/home/student-id-card.css owns import order, while src/styles/themes/bright-school/home/student-id-card/ contains card-shell-avatar, identity-name, user-identity-tag, and mode-stats rules.
  • Bright School commerce shop polish is a nested import-only entry through src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/shop.css; window-redesign.css owns the paper shop window, semantic header, product-stage cards, wallet, dialogue, and portrait composition, while card-badges.css owns the late-theme category and quantity corner badges without growing the main owner past the CSS size contract. Desktop reserves the upper-left stage for at most five cards in balanced 2+3 / 2+2 / 2+1 rows, with seeded jitter confined to 28px-safe cells; lower-left remains the wallet and lower-right remains Zahira. Dialogue is bottom-anchored above the rendered portrait instead of sticking to the header, so its tail stays visually connected without covering cards or art. Card rotation is fixed per batch while the transform-only vertical float pauses on hover, focus, press, and purchase and is removed by reduced-motion.
  • Bright School commerce warehouse/profile polish is also a nested import-only entry: src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/warehouse-profile.css owns import order while src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/warehouse-profile/ contains typography-numbers, profile-stats, warehouse-header, warehouse-empty, warehouse-item-card, and warehouse-item-mobile rules.
  • Bright School hard-shadow cards inside scrollable or clipped modal regions reserve right/bottom bleed on their content owners rather than weakening the shadows. The house manual character list, leaderboard list and pinned current row, warehouse grid, shop layout/sidebar lane, and friends list each keep explicit desktop/mobile padding or scroll-padding so right and lower shadows are not cut at the modal edge.
  • Startorch HUD hardening is also a nested import-only entry: src/styles/hud-components/hud-hardening.css owns import order while src/styles/hud-components/hud-hardening/ contains tokens-shell-scrollbars, inputs-settings-auth, inventory-state-tags, home-hologram-entries, character-deploy-detail, shop-pagination-owned, warehouse-surfaces, and friend-actions rules.
  • Shared UserIdentity cosmetics are also nested import-only through src/styles/hud-components/user-identity.css, delegating fixed-ratio nameplate primitives, surface-specific alignment hooks, and phone scale hooks to src/styles/hud-components/user-identity/ while keeping the shared HUD cascade order stable.
  • Shared mobile battle shell/dock styling is also a nested import-only entry: src/styles/mobile-room/base-shell-dock.css owns import order while src/styles/mobile-room/base-shell-dock/ contains shell-header-menu, flat-control-reset, viewport-dock-shell, player-timer-strip, board-viewport, dock-tabs-actions, and decision-chat-panel rules.
  • Startorch terminal modal system styling is also a nested import-only entry: src/styles/modals/terminal-system.css owns import order while src/styles/modals/terminal-system/ contains tokens-backdrop, modal-chrome, close-header-actions, terminal-buttons, replay-profile-surfaces, result-modal, and outcomes-resume-actions rules.
  • Bright School portrait mobile house/profile styling is also a nested import-only entry: src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/house-profile.css owns import order while src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/house-profile/ contains shell-profile-stats, character-grid-cards, owned-decorations, and character-detail-music rules.
  • 部员手册角色详情的共享 BGM 控件使用紧凑内联结构：凸起播放键、只显示当前曲名的单行标题和浮动选曲纸页在所有主题下保持相同行为；普通技/派生技身份只在选曲纸页页签中出现，不占用闭合标题空间。Bright School 的闭合控件不再绘制 Rough.js 外框、重复按钮环或卡片硬阴影，而由 44px 触控区内的圆角方形硬件键、普通 UI 字体和一条克制的 Rough.js 标题下划线组成，让周围已有的校园方格纸承担主题氛围。外层 44px 按钮在 default/hover/focus/active 都强制透明，避免通用 button:hover 把整个点击区染粉；只有内缩 32px 键面变色，默认透明、悬停/焦点绿色、按下红色。悬停抬高 1px，按下时下沉 2px并缩短底座阴影，不再用整体缩放制造点击反馈。共享 modals/character-music-player.css 与主题 component-repairs/character-music-player.css 都是 import-only 入口，分别把外壳/标题、选曲纸页、动效和 Bright School 外壳/纸页皮肤分到同名子目录，避免播放器迭代重新形成超大 CSS 文件。曲名只在溢出时单向自动滚动，选曲纸页内只有当前 hover/focus 的溢出行滚动；派生技与普通技通过页签明确分组。桌面和手机继续把播放器内联放在角色标题旁，手机宽度收束但播放键保持 44px 触控目标，选曲纸页使用视口约束和内部四行滚动而不把详情弹窗撑宽。
  • Bright School portrait mobile commerce and warehouse layout remains a nested import-only entry, but the shop's final post-theme shell contract lives in src/styles/mobile-adaptive/shop-window-redesign.css, shop-window-card-layout.css owns final card-size isolation and internal density, shop-window-compact.css owns the under-620px reception-area compression, and src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/shop/background-crayon.css owns the themed background layers without expanding the structural window file. The shell owner explicitly clears shared modal padding/gap and gives the header/body the full paper width instead of using negative-margin expansion. The shop body owns a restrained crayon campus-store background: a pale-blue display wall, mint reception wall, and warm-brown counter are rendered as static CSS layers with low-frequency translucent strokes, paper-fiber highlights, and irregular clipped edges instead of flat bands, repeated stripe textures, runtime SVG/noise filters, animation, or a new raster dependency. Desktop keeps the display boundary around the 68% horizontal product lane; final mobile CSS changes the display-wall clip polygon to a slightly uneven 56% horizontal edge matching the product-stage height. The card owner beats the portrait-wide max-width: 100% guard, legacy shop-card minimum heights, centered shrink-to-content grid columns, item-price right alignment, and inherited action sizing with .shop-window-scoped rules, so scale is applied once, the rendered card stays within its algorithmic slot, every purchase action fills the same content width, and the price element itself is centered. The product stage never scrolls; five cards use centered 2+3, four use 2+2, and three use 2+1. Horizontal geometry keeps 4–5px gaps, while the vertical layout reserves rotation, float, and hard-shadow bleed so the visible clearance remains similar and the bottom purchase controls are not clipped; enlarged product art, compact name/price rows, the bottom purchase action, and both corner badges still scale as one unit with no fixed minimum. The shared 4–6px float parameter is doubled only for desktop into an 8–12px total travel; mobile resets it to 4–6px and reduced-motion still disables continuous floating. Dialogue and wallet sit below the products, the wallet stays clear of Zahira's visible hair, and the compact owner scales the reception assets apart on short screens; refresh/close retain 44px controls and safe-area clearance.

Modal Layering

  • Shared .modal-backdrop stacks above room --room-floating-z surfaces so request and confirmation modals dim skill chips, chat controls, and room member popovers together.
  • Mobile home and room floating controls use explicit local z-index tokens instead of ad hoc fixed layer numbers. Bright School portrait home sets --home-floating-z: 120 on .home-top-strip and applies it to .home-mobile-menu plus .home-mobile-menu-panel, keeping the folded action menu above the home main panel. Mobile battle rooms set --room-floating-z: 120 in base and final mobile layers; opened room menus, chat widgets, chat popovers, and .mobile-room-dock:has(.chat-widget.open) use that token while preserving modal-backdrop overrides that intentionally lower room controls under active modals.
  • Player story playback uses a dedicated top modal layer on .onboarding-story-backdrop, above ordinary .modal-backdrop sheets and the final mobile modal safeguard, so item-triggered stories remain clickable while the warehouse or other business modal stays underneath. Story dismissal is centralized: opening the generic player clears the legacy onboarding-story flag, and closing/skipping/back dismissal clears both story overlay flags plus the active script. Admin embedded story previews reset that backdrop to normal document stacking with z-index: auto.
  • Mobile room portraits do not render character-chain badges; duplicate-chain data remains in user payloads but is hidden on player-facing portrait surfaces.
  • Battle result modals are player-centered. src/modals/gameLifecycle/ResultModal.jsx keeps winner color only for result semantics, but the portrait, candy item effects, chain badge, and UserIdentity come from the current user's room player when available. The modal emits result-outcome-win, result-outcome-loss, result-outcome-draw, or result-outcome-spectator: win shows 赢了耶！ with the existing portrait hop, loss shows 输掉了... with a grayscale frozen portrait plus /assets/effects/result-defeat-rain.gif, and draw/observer states keep the panel compact without victory motion.
  • Desktop, Startorch terminal, Bright School, and final mobile result styles must keep the outcome label first and keep loss rain sized from --result-defeat-weather-width/top. Result reward tiles are intentionally filled rather than transparent: result-reward-negative uses a pale red surface, result-reward-nonnegative uses pale green, and result-reward-coins uses pale gold, with terminal/Bright School/mobile overrides preserving those semantic fills. The inner .text-rating-value in result rewards keeps only the semantic numeric font and must not draw its own background, border, or shadow. Mobile result sheets collapse to one column, cap the portrait through --result-portrait-size, and center the confirm action without reintroducing a winner-only layout.

Feedback Messages

  • Player feedback is persisted in the FeedbackMessage Prisma model, backed by the 202605250001_add_feedback_message migration.
  • Logged-in users submit feedback through POST /api/feedback; the server stores userId, current username, normalized content, and createdAt.
  • Feedback content strips control characters, trims whitespace, rejects empty submissions, and caps content at 400 characters.
  • The home and room message-board modal calls the feedback API. On success it closes the modal and shows a green top toast: 感谢您的反馈！.
  • The message-board textarea uses the same 400-character limit, displays the remaining character count below the input, and defaults to a taller writing area on desktop while mobile clamps the height to the viewport.
  • Admins can view the latest 100 feedback messages through GET /api/admin/feedback.
  • The admin console includes a 留言反馈 tab that displays submit time, username, and full feedback content with wrapping for longer text.

Its button uses five authored colors around a light, stable, dark-text inner surface, with slow irregular background-position drift and a static readable reduced-motion fallback; normal item ordering remains poster, memorial ticket, radio. Starting it mounts a body portal above all registered modal layers, blocks pointer/touch/keyboard and app-level dismissal, and still runs the full authored presentation under prefers-reduced-motion because the sequence conveys the required state transition without strobing. The page begins gradually darkening with the first 999:00 frame; at 3.2 seconds the eight-frame Pink Cyber Angel right-flight loop enters from outside the left edge on a deterministic irregular vertical path, settles over the countdown at 5.2 seconds, switches to a four-frame wave, and glows at 5.8 seconds. The flash uses the same measured target coordinates as the settled sprite, first appearing as a local orb at the character rather than at viewport center, then reaching a fully opaque cover for the 6.25-second sprite/countdown swap. White and dim layers fade away by 7.05 seconds and input unlocks; local completion is remembered separately from interruption, while a receipt-anchored display deadline preserves roughly four visible seconds on portrait mobile even after a slow start response and transitions directly to ready at zero without a refetch wait. The server readyAt remains the claim gate. Desktop and portrait mobile remeasure viewport/countdown coordinates on resize, and only page hide/offline/unmount removes the body lock before requesting server interruption.

Player cards remain structural aside elements, portrait buttons own viewpoint switching, and mobile battle UserIdentity rules override ellipsis only in this surface: legal names remain one line, legacy names wrap completely, and equipped title/badge/nameplate visuals stay present.

When a concrete yellow target ring is visible, wrong move/skill point input always reports 请落子或选择黄圈位置; hidden-target exercises preserve their authored wrong-point copy. If a hidden-target move enters an NPC wrong-answer branch, the runtime waits until the typewriter has completed, keeps the full line visible for another 1.5 seconds, then advances automatically even when legacy node data says immediate or manual continuation.
  • MobileRoomLayout is now a shell only. RoomBattleStage owns the rendered mobile battle grid, player strips, board slot, and bottom tab dock, so the layout no longer tries to extract composite React children before they render. The mobile action dock keeps player actions in one five-button touch row with 44px-plus targets, short visible text labels, and a bounded operation-hint region, while replay/spectator history uses a separate seven-slot icon row for first, minus-five, previous, centered icon-free move count, next, plus-five, and last. Bright School player-facing internal scroll owners hide scrollbar chrome with scrollbar-width: none / WebKit scrollbar suppression while keeping overflow-y: auto, touch momentum, and containment. Theme and HUD motion should animate transform, opacity, color, or shadow only; progress/timer bars must not declare transition: width, and spring/bounce cubic-bezier curves are avoided in favor of smooth deceleration. Mobile and Bright School overrides must preserve the existing TimeBar grid, digit, and track contract instead of adding tutorial-specific centering overrides.
  • The shared preload stage uses the same 16px orange-gradient track and 48px aspect-preserving endpoint mascot on desktop and mobile. Fill growth uses scaleX() rather than a width transition; clipped dots and a travelling highlight create visible motion inside the loaded area, and a blurred warm glow scales from the same origin without drawing an outer capsule edge. The small mascot remains upright while its anchor follows the real endpoint with an ease-out transition, and the track reserves enough horizontal room to keep it inside narrow screens. When progress is unchanged or advances by at most one point the mascot floats up to four pixels; meaningful movement switches to a restrained two-degree glide until 600ms of inactivity. Bright School may retain its paper track and dark outline, but its late meter rules must resolve --preload-progress-fill to the owner layered gradient instead of reusing the timer's pink fill. Reduced-motion removes interpolation, travelling highlight, glide, and idle float without moving the mascot or static texture away from the current progress endpoint.

玩家铭牌内部保持头像、用户名、段位面板三列固定结构，用户名只能在中间列内缩放和收敛，不能跨列遮挡段位面板；铭牌外壳背景使用 public/assets/home/student-id-nameplate.webp 手绘学生证图，PNG 同目录保留为源图，背景按 border-box 铺满整张卡片且不再叠旧粉绿渐变，图片只提供外框和三块安全区，头像、用户名和模式图标/段位由 DOM/CSS
